## Seeds Regulations {#seeds-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 1400
:::

[SEEDS ACT](/eng/acts/S-8)

Regulations Respecting the Quality of Seeds Including Seed Potatoes, and
the Testing, Inspection, and Sale Thereof
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-510302 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Seeds Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1400).

**[[1.01 and 1.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.01_and_1.1
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/96-252, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-510305 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in these Regulations.

  [[biotechnology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[biotechnology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the application of
      science and engineering to the direct or indirect use of living
      organisms or parts or products of living organisms in their
      natural or modified forms; ([biotechnologie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[conformity verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[conformity verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      independent body that has an agreement with the Agency under
      subsection 14(1) of the [Canadian Food Inspection Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5) to administer specific tasks, including
      assessing, recommending for acceptance and subsequent audit of
      establishments, operators, graders, samplers or laboratories that
      process, import, sample, test, grade or label seeds. ([organisme
      de vérification de la conformité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes seed derived through
      biotechnology. ([semences]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Seeds Fees Notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Seeds Fees Notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-223, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The definitions in this subsection apply in Parts I,
  III and IV.

  [[accredited grader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[accredited grader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is
      accredited pursuant to section 13.1; ([classificateur
      agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Seeds
      Act](/eng/acts/S-8); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Food Inspection
      Agency established by section 3 of the [Canadian Food Inspection
      Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5); ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Seed
      Growers' Association; ([Association]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada pedigreed grade name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada pedigreed grade name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      designation that contains one of the words
      [[foundation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
      [[registered]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[certified]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}; ([dénomination de la catégorie
      Canada généalogique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian Methods and Procedures for Testing Seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian Methods and Procedures for Testing
      Seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the methods and procedures for
      testing seed set out in the Canadian Methods and Procedures for
      Testing Seed prepared by the Agency, as amended from time to time;
      ([méthodes et procédés canadiens d'essai des
      semences]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian Wheat Board Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian Wheat Board Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that part
      of Canada comprising

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provinces of Manitoba, Saskatchewan and
        Alberta, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those parts of British Columbia known as the
        Peace River District and the Creston-Wynndell Area; ([territoire
        de la Commission canadienne du blé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[certified status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[certified status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed,
      means that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a crop certificate designated "Certified" has
          been issued for that crop by the Association, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the Association for seed of that kind or
          species, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was not grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the crop meets the standards established by
          an official certifying agency and approved by the Association,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the official certifying agency for seed
          of that kind or species and approved by the Association;
          ([qualité Certifiée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed, means to
      prepare by cleaning, processing, packing, treating or changing in
      any other manner the nature of a seed lot;
      ([conditionner]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crop certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crop certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document that is
      issued by the Association certifying the crop identified therein
      has met the standards for varietal purity established by the
      Association for crops of that kind or species; ([certificat de
      récolte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[domestic tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an official tag in
      respect of seed that is derived from a crop grown in Canada and
      graded with a Canada pedigreed grade name; ([étiquette
      canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fastened]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fastened]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a package, means
      sealed in such a manner that it is impossible to open the package
      easily without leaving evidence of it having been opened;
      ([scellé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[flower seeds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flower seeds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means seeds of herbaceous
      plants grown for their blooms, ornamental foliage or other
      ornamental parts; ([semences de fleurs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[foundation status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foundation status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed,
      means that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a crop certificate designated "Foundation"
          has been issued for that crop by the Association, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the Association for seed of that kind or
          species, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was not grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the crop meets the standards established by
          an official certifying agency and approved by the Association,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the official certifying agency for seed
          of that kind or species and approved by the Association;
          ([qualité Fondation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[grade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[grade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed, means to
      determine, establish or assign a grade name to seed based on the
      analysis of a representative sample of the seed lot and the seed
      standards prescribed by sections 6 and 7;
      ([classer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inter-agency certification tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inter-agency certification tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      official tag in respect of seed that is certified by an official
      certifying agency and graded with a Canada pedigreed grade name;
      ([étiquette de certification inter-agences]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[licensed sampler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licensed sampler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      licensed under subsection 13.1(2.1) to sample seed;
      ([échantillonneur agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[lot designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lot designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a number, mark,
      symbol or test number that identifies a seed lot; ([désignation de
      lot]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[lot number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lot number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in the case of seed graded
      with one of the Canada Registered or Canada Foundation grade
      names, includes the pedigreed seed declaration number; ([numéro de
      lot]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[official certifying agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[official certifying agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      organization in a foreign state that is empowered by the
      government of that state to certify seed crops and seed as to the
      varietal purity thereof where that state is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a participating member of the Organisation for
        Economic Co-operation and Development's schemes for the varietal
        certification of seed moving in international trade for that
        kind or species, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a member of the Association of Official Seed
        Certifying Agencies; ([agence officielle de
        certification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[official sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[official sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a sample of seed
      that has been drawn by an inspector; ([échantillon
      officiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[official tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[official tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label that is issued
      or approved by the Agency in respect of seed having pedigreed
      status. ([étiquette officielle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officially recognized laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially recognized laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      seed testing laboratory that is designated by the Minister as an
      accredited laboratory under section 2.1 of the Act; ([laboratoire
      reconnu officiellement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officially-recognized laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially-recognized laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[
      \[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[officially recognized sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially recognized sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      sample of seed that has been

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} drawn by or under the supervision of a licensed
        sampler and prepared by a licensed sampler according to
        recognized standard methods; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} drawn and prepared according to recognized
        standard methods by an individual accredited to do so by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a government body, or an officially
          recognized body, that has the authority to regulate the
          import, export or marketing of seed,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an official certifying agency, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a seed testing laboratory accredited by the
          International Seed Testing Association (ISTA) to issue
          international seed lot certificates, if the sample is to be
          tested by the accrediting laboratory or by another laboratory
          accredited by ISTA. ([échantillon reconnu
          officiellement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officially-recognized sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially-recognized sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-223, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[officially recognized test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially recognized test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a test
      that is performed according to recognized standard methods on an
      officially recognized sample in an officially recognized
      laboratory or, in the case of determinations of impurities in the
      kinds or species set out in Tables I to VI of Schedule I or seeds
      of similar size, an accredited grader. ([essai reconnu
      officiellement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officially-recognized test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officially-recognized test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-223, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[pedigreed seed declaration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pedigreed seed declaration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the form
      supplied by the Agency relating to the declaration of processing
      of seed of pedigreed status; ([déclaration de semence
      généalogique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pedigreed seed declaration number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pedigreed seed declaration number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the number on the pedigreed seed declaration; ([numéro de la
      déclaration de semence généalogique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pedigreed status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pedigreed status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed,
      means that the seed is of foundation status, registered status or
      certified status or the seed is approved by the Association as
      being breeder seed or select seed; ([qualité
      Généalogique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pedigreed status tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pedigreed status tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an official tag
      in respect of seed that is derived from a crop that was grown in
      Canada and not graded; ([étiquette de qualité
      Généalogique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[PPTM varietal blend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[PPTM varietal blend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a varietal blend
      for plant pest tolerance management that is intended to maintain
      the efficacy of a plant pest tolerance characteristic; ([mélange
      de variétés GRP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the President of the
      Agency; ([président]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[progeny]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[progeny]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means seed or seed-like crops and
      includes cereal grains, oilseeds and pulses;
      ([descendance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recognized standard method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recognized standard method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Canadian Methods and Procedures for Testing
        Seed,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Rules for Testing Seed, published by the
        Association of Official Seed Analysts, as amended from time to
        time, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} International Rules for Seed Testing, published
        by the International Seed Testing Association, as amended from
        time to time; ([méthode normalisée reconnue]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[registered status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed,
      means that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a crop certificate designated "Registered"
          has been issued for that crop by the Association, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the Association for seed of that kind or
          species, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the crop from which the seed is derived
        was not grown in Canada,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the crop meets the standards established by
          an official certifying agency and approved by the Association,
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the standards for varietal
          purity established by the official certifying agency for seed
          of that kind or species and approved by the Association;
          ([qualité Enregistrée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[seed lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seed lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a quantity of seed, each
      portion of which is, within reasonable limits, uniform with
      respect to purity, germination, variety, type and quality; ([lot
      de semence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specialty seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specialty seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means seed of a kind or
      species or of a mixture of kinds or species mixed with or attached
      to any fertilizer, soil, compost, peat, moss, mica, plastic,
      paper, cellulose or other material and includes artificial seeds;
      ([semence spéciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[varietal blend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[varietal blend]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture of two or
      more varieties of the same kind or species of pedigreed status
      seed; ([mélange de variétés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[variety]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[variety]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned to
      [[cultivar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} by the International Union of
      Biological Sciences' Commission for the Nomenclature of Cultivated
      Plants and denotes an assemblage of cultivated plants, including
      hybrids constituted by controlled cross-pollination, that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are distinguished by common morphological,
        physiological, cytological, chemical or other characteristics,
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} retain their distinguishing characteristics
        when reproduced; ([variété]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[variety name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[variety name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a word, a number or
      a letter and number combination used to designate a variety. ([nom
      de variété]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-108, s. 401\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-108, s. 401\]]{.Repealed}

## [Services, Rights, Privileges and Use of Facilities]{.HTitleText1} {#h-510395 .Part}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** No service, right,
privilege or use of a facility under these Regulations shall be provided
or granted to a person who requests it unless the person includes the
applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
Notice with the application or request or has agreed in writing to pay
the fee on receipt of an invoice from the Agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-534, s. 2
- SOR/2000-183, s. 38
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Seeds other than Seed Potatoes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-510399 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510400 .Subheading}

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person designated by the
President to accredit graders or license samplers.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-223, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510404 .Subheading}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply in
respect of seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 1
- SOR/82-437, s. 1
- SOR/86-849, s. 1
- SOR/86-850, s. 2
- SOR/88-242, s. 1
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510408 .Subheading}

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  approved by the Association as breeder seed or select seed is exempt
  from the operation of paragraph 3(1)(a) of the Act in so far as it may
  be sold in Canada without conforming to the requirements set out in
  section 6, 9, 16, 18, 19 and 23 to 31.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Seed that is labelled for export is exempt from the
  operation of paragraph 3(1)(a) of the Act in so far as it may be
  exported from Canada without conforming to the requirements set out in
  sections 6, 7, 9, 10, 16, 18, 19, subsection 20(5) and sections 21 and
  23 to 31.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Seed that is imported into, or sold in, Canada for
  the purpose of conditioning, that is accompanied by a label or other
  documentation that shows that the seed is ungraded and is being
  imported or sold only for that purpose is exempt from the operation of
  paragraph 3(1)(a) of the Act in so far as it may be imported into or
  sold in Canada without conforming to the standards set out in sections
  6 and 23 to 31.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Seed of any variety is exempt from the operation of
  paragraph 3(1)(b) of the Act in so far as it may be sold or advertised
  for sale without being registered if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed is of pedigreed status;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seed is labelled in accordance with section 35;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the seed is to be sold

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the production of pedigreed seed, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the variety is entered in variety
      registration trials, for the production of material for evaluation
      of its suitability for processing;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the seed is sold pursuant to a contract that
    specifies that in the case of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} seed sold for the production of pedigreed seed,
      all of the progeny will be delivered to a destination specified in
      the contract, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} varieties that are entered into variety
      registration trials, all of the progeny will be delivered to an
      industrial mill or plant for the sole purpose of evaluating the
      variety for its suitability for processing; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} all of the progeny of the seed is delivered to the
    destination specified in the contract referred to in paragraph (d).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Seeds of vegetables, roots and herbs are exempt from
  the operation of paragraph 3(1)(a) of the Act in so far as they may be
  sold without a grade name and without conforming with the standards
  for minimum percentage of germination under section 6 if they are
  labelled in accordance with section 30.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Seed of a kind or species set out in Schedule I that
  is imported for research purposes is exempt from the operation of
  paragraph 3(1)(a) of the Act in so far as it may be imported into
  Canada without conforming with the standards for minimum percentage of
  germination set out in that Schedule.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

### [Standards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510428 .Subheading}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (3), the standards for seed of the kinds and species set
  out in Schedule I and the grade names therefor are as set out in that
  Schedule.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Seed of a kind or species not set out in Schedule I
  shall meet the minimum weed seed and other crop seed standards set out
  in the following Tables of Schedule I:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species with 15 or fewer seeds per
    gram, Table V;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species with 16 to 50 seeds per
    gram, Table II;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species with 51 to 250 seeds per
    gram, Table IV;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species with 251 to 600 seeds per
    gram, Table VIII;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species with 601 to 1,500 seeds per
    gram, Table IX;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species of grass with 1,500 or
    fewer seeds per gram, Table XI;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for any kind or species of grass with 1,501 seeds
    or more per gram, Table XII;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for any seed or mixture of seeds for land
    reclamation, soil conservation, green cover, wildlife grazing or
    habitat, wetland restoration and similar purposes, Table XIII;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for herbs and vegetables, Table XX, unless they
    have 1,000 or more seeds per gram, in which case Table XII shall be
    used; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} wildflower mixtures and similar products intended
    for landscape gardening use, Table XV.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Barley seed that has been treated with a product
  registered as a control product under the [Pest Control Products
  Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01) for the control of true loose smut ([Ustilago
  nuda]{.otherLang lang="la"}) is exempt from the standards for true
  loose smut set out in column 9 of Table II to Schedule I.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Seed referred to in subsection (2) shall not be
  considered a weed seed for the purpose of that subsection.

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to the standards prescribed by section 6, the following
  standards apply to seed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed shall not contain prohibited noxious weed
    seeds;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the seed shall have been subject to appropriate
    mixing, blending and processing techniques so that it is as uniform
    as practicable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if graded with the name of one of the Canada
    Foundation grades, the seed shall be of foundation status and, if
    two or more seed lots of the same variety are mixed, a new crop
    certificate is required to be issued by the Association if the seed
    meets the standards established by the Association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if graded with the name of one of the Canada
    Registered grades, the seed shall be of foundation status or
    registered status and, if two or more seed lots of the same variety
    are mixed, a new crop certificate is required to be issued by the
    Association if the seed meets the standards established by the
    Association;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if graded with the name of one of the Canada
    Certified grades, the seed shall be of foundation status, registered
    status or certified status and, if two or more seed lots of the same
    variety are mixed, the Agency shall be so informed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if graded with the name of one of the varietal
    blend grades, all of the seed shall be of pedigreed status;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a Canada Certified No. 1 Forage Mixture or a Canada
    Certified No. 2 Forage Mixture may contain more than one variety of
    a kind or species;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} every Canada Certified No. 1 Cereal Mixture and
    Canada Certified No. 2 Cereal Mixture shall contain only seed of
    pedigreed status and only one variety per kind or species; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a Canada Certified No. 1 Lawn Mixture or a Canada
    Certified No. 2 Lawn Mixture may contain more than one variety of a
    kind or species.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table I to Schedule I, Canada Foundation
  No. 2 seed and Canada Registered No. 2 seed may contain one secondary
  noxious weed seed per 10 kg.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table I to Schedule I, Canada Certified No.
  2 seed may contain one secondary noxious weed seed per 2 kg.

- [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table II to Schedule I, Canada Foundation
  No. 2 oats and Canada Registered No. 2 oats may contain one secondary
  noxious weed seed per 10 kg.

- [(1.4)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table II to Schedule I, Canada Certified
  No. 1 oats may contain one secondary noxious weed seed per 2 kg.

- [(1.5)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table II to Schedule I, Canada Foundation
  No. 2 seed other than oats, Canada Registered No. 2 seed other than
  oats and Canada Certified No. 1 seed other than oats may contain one
  secondary noxious weed seed per 5 kg.

- [(1.6)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table III to Schedule I, Canada Certified
  No. 1 Cereal Mixture seed may contain one secondary noxious weed seed
  per 5 kg.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to meeting the standards set out in
  Tables I to III to Schedule I, seed to which those Tables apply shall
  be free from tartarian buckwheat in Manitoba, Saskatchewan, Alberta
  and British Columbia.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to meeting the standards set out in
  Tables I to III to Schedule I, seed to which those Tables apply shall
  be free from wild oats in Quebec, Nova Scotia, New Brunswick and
  Prince Edward Island.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table IV to Schedule I, Canada Foundation No.
  2 seed, Canada Registered No. 2 seed and Canada Certified No. 1 seed
  may contain one secondary noxious weed seed per 50 g other than wild
  oats in flax.

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table IV to Schedule I, Canada Foundation
  No. 1 seed and Canada Registered No. 1 seed of sorghum, Sudan grass
  and canarygrass may contain one other crop seed per 25 g.

- [(4.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table V to Schedule I, Canada Foundation
  No. 2 seed may contain one other crop seed per 2 kg.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Seed to which Table VII to Schedule I applies shall
  be free from seeds of cleavers ([Galium aparine]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  L.) and false cleavers ([Galium spurium]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.).

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Column 5 of Table VIII to Schedule I does not apply
  to sweet clover seed.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the kinds and species of seeds set
  out in Table XI to Schedule I,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 1 of column 10 of that Table applies only
    to Chewing's fescue, meadow fescue, red fescue, creeping red fescue,
    tall fescue, annual ryegrass, intermediate ryegrass and perennial
    ryegrass;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 2 of column 10 of that Table applies only
    to smooth bromegrass, meadow bromegrass, sweet bromegrass,
    fine-leaved fescue, hard fescue, sheep fescue, various-leaved
    fescue, creeping foxtail, meadow foxtail, orchardgrass, beardless
    wheatgrass, northern wheatgrass, pubescent wheatgrass, slender
    wheatgrass, streambank wheatgrass, Altai wildrye, Dahurian wildrye
    and Russian wildrye;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 3 of column 10 of that Table applies only
    to crested wheatgrass, intermediate wheatgrass, Siberian wheatgrass,
    tall wheatgrass, western wheatgrass and tall oatgrass;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 4 of column 10 of that Table applies only
    to reed canarygrass;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 1 of column 11 of that Table applies only
    to Chewing's fescue, meadow fescue, red fescue, creeping red fescue,
    tall fescue, annual ryegrass, intermediate ryegrass, perennial
    ryegrass and reed canarygrass; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subcolumn 2 of column 11 of that Table applies only
    to smooth bromegrass, meadow bromegrass, sweet bromegrass,
    fine-leaved fescue, hard fescue, sheep fescue, various-leaved
    fescue, creeping foxtail, meadow foxtail, tall oatgrass,
    orchardgrass, beardless wheatgrass, crested wheatgrass, intermediate
    wheatgrass, northern wheatgrass, pubescent wheatgrass, Siberian
    wheatgrass, slender wheatgrass, streambank wheatgrass, tall
    wheatgrass, western wheatgrass, Altai wildrye, Dahurian wildrye and
    Russian wildrye.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Despite the kinds and species of seed set out in
  Table XIII to Schedule I, column 5 of that Table does not apply to
  mixtures that contain 1% or more of sweet clover seed.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding Table XIII to Schedule I, Common No.
  1 Forage Mixture may contain one primary noxious weed per 25 g where
  there is present, singly or combined, 10 per cent or more of common
  timothy, dwarf timothy, Canada bluegrass, Kentucky bluegrass or redtop
  seed.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding Table XIV to Schedule I, all
  mixtures described on a label to be suitable for shady places shall
  contain not less than 40 per cent by weight, singly or combined, of
  Chewing's fescue, creeping red fescue or rough bluegrass seed.

- [(10.1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the standards set out in Tables III
  and XV to Schedule I, kinds or species of seed listed in Schedule I
  shall meet the minimum percentage of germination set out in the Table
  in which they appear.

- [(10.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite Table XVIII to Schedule I, Canada
  Foundation No. 2 seed may contain one other crop seed per 2 kg.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} The diameter of sets set out in column 2 of Table
  XXI to Schedule I do not apply to multiplier onions.

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of Table XXI to Schedule I, a
  tolerance of three per cent by weight is allowed for the oversized or
  undersized sets for each grade of onion sets.

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** Seed of any variety of
field corn shall not be sold in Canada unless it is of pedigreed status.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Any seed that is a
component of specialty seed shall meet the standards set out in sections
6 and 7 before being mixed with or attached to non-seed material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Use of Variety Names]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510492 .Subheading}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, unless the seed is of that variety,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} use or allow to be used the variety name on any
    label or package of seed or in any invoice, circular or advertising
    related to seed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} otherwise represent any seed to be of a specified
    variety.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use or allow to be used a modified or
  qualified variety name in respect of seed of that variety.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use a variety name, or allow a
  variety name to be used, on any label or package of seed of a kind or
  species set out in Schedule II or in any invoice, circular or
  advertising related to seed of that kind or species unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} except as provided in subsection (4), the seed is
    graded with a Canada pedigreed grade name and labelled under
    sections 32 to 37 when sold;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a mixture or varietal blend,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the mixture or varietal blend was made by an
      approved conditioner registered under Part IV and all of the seed
      named as to variety is of pedigreed status; or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the mixture or varietal blend is imported,
      the seed is accompanied at the time of importation by a
      certificate of an official certifying agency confirming that all
      of the seed named as to variety is of pedigreed status; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the seed is of a vegetable-type variety.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Seed of a kind set out in Schedule II may be sold by
  variety name for the purpose of conditioning if the seed is of
  pedigreed status and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the seed is not in fastened packages, the seed
    is accompanied by the grower's declaration referred to in paragraph
    13(1)(c) and the seed moves

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} directly from the grower of the seed to an
      approved conditioner registered under Part IV, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} between approved conditioners registered under
      Part IV; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the seed is in fastened packages, a pedigreed
    status tag is attached to each package.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Seed loses its pedigreed status when

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sealed packages are opened elsewhere than in an
    approved conditioner registered under Part IV;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seed moves in unsealed packages to a place that
    is neither an approved conditioner nor a bulk storage facility
    registered under Part IV;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Association withdraws the crop certificate that
    was issued for the crop from which the seed is derived; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the seed has been contaminated such that it does
    not meet the standards for varietal purity established by the
    Association.

### [Seed Testing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510516 .Subheading}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of determining whether the seed meets the standards set
  out in sections 6 and 7, the seed shall be subject to the following
  tests

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed to be graded with a Canada
    pedigreed grade name, other than seed corn, an officially recognized
    test;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of corn to be graded with a Canada
    pedigreed grade name, non-pedigreed seed of the kinds or species set
    out in Tables VII to XII to Schedule I, and for the purposes of
    subsection 40(2), a recognized standard method by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an officially recognized laboratory,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of determinations of impurities in
      the kinds or species set out in Tables I to VI of Schedule I or
      seeds of a similar size, an accredited grader,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a seed testing laboratory operating under the
      supervision of a Senior or Associate member of the Commercial Seed
      Analysts' Association of Canada,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a seed testing laboratory operating under the
      supervision of a Registered Seed Technologist registered by the
      Society of Commercial Seed Technologists,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a seed testing laboratory operated by or under
      the authority of a national or state government of a foreign
      country, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a seed testing laboratory accredited by the
      International Seed Testing Association (ISTA); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other seed, a recognized
    standard method.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Barley seed that has been labelled to indicate that
  it has been neither tested nor treated for true loose smut is exempt
  from being tested for true loose smut.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2003-6, s. 104
- SOR/2007-223, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sampling of Seed]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510531 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Samples of seed for the testing
  referred to in section 11 shall be taken in conformity with recognized
  standard methods.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a sample of seed submitted
  for testing shall be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where one gram of the seed lot contains 5,000 or
    more seeds, not less than 10 g;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where one gram of the seed lot contains more than
    25 seeds but less than 5,000 seeds, not less than 50,000 seeds; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where one gram of the seed lot contains 25 or less
    seeds, not less than 2 kg.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A working sample for determination of impurities
  shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of grading those kinds and species
    set out in Schedule I, the quantity indicated in Tables 1 and 2 of
    the Canadian Methods and Procedures for Testing Seed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for all other purposes, one half of the quantity
    set out in subsection (2).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (2) and (3) do not apply if the sample is
  required for the purpose of checking only the percentage of
  germination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2003-6, s. 105
- SOR/2007-223, s. 7(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Grading of Seed]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510544 .Subheading}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as provided in subsections (2) and (3), seed may be graded with a
  Canada pedigreed grade name only where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed is of pedigreed status;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seed has been conditioned by the grower of the
    seed on the grower's premises or by an approved conditioner
    registered pursuant to Part IV;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the grower of the seed has completed and signed a
    declaration attesting that the seed is derived from a crop that has
    been issued a crop certificate and that the seed has not been
    contaminated by any other seed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the seed is graded by an accredited grader, based
    on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the results of an officially recognized test, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed corn, a test done in
      accordance with paragraph 11(1)(b), and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a varietal blend, the blend is a
    PPTM varietal blend of kinds or species set out in any of Tables I
    to II.1 and IV to VII of Schedule I.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where seeds are not conditioned on the grower's
  premises and it can be determined that the services of an approved
  conditioner are not available, a grower may, under the supervision of
  an inspector, have seed conditioned by other than an approved
  conditioner.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Imported seed may be graded with a Canada pedigreed
  grade name only where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed meets the condition set out in paragraph
    1(d); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seed is imported in a package bearing a label
    that was issued or approved by an official certifying agency and
    that indicates that the seed is of pedigreed status.

### [Samplers and Graders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510560 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2003-6, s. 106

\]
:::

- **[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any person who wishes to be
  accredited as a grader or licensed as a sampler shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply for the accreditation or the licence in
    writing to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a conformity verification body, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar, if there is no conformity
      verification body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} submit to an evaluation that is set by the
    Registrar and that measures knowledge of the principles and
    practices for the conditioning, sampling, testing and grading of
    seed of pedigreed status;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the individual wishes to be licensed to
    sample seed, submit to an evaluation that is set by the Registrar
    and that measures the individual's ability to so sample;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} where the individual wishes to be accredited to
    retrieve, identify, classify and report weed seeds and other
    impurities from officially recognized samples, submit to an
    evaluation that is set by the Registrar and that measures the
    individual's ability to so retrieve, identify, classify and report;

  - [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} where the individual wishes to be accredited to
    grade seed, submit to an evaluation that is set by the Registrar and
    that measures the individual's ability to grade seed; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} where the individual wishes to be accredited to
    evaluate imported seed and accompanying documents for conformity
    with these Regulations, submit to an evaluation that is set by the
    Registrar that measures an individual's ability to so evaluate.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} When making an application under subparagraph
  (1)(a)(ii), the applicant shall include the applicable fee set out in
  the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the individual has obtained a mark of at least 80%
  on the evaluation referred to in paragraph (1)(b) and at least 80% on
  the applicable evaluation referred to in paragraph (1)(c.1), (c.2) or
  (d), the Registrar shall accredit the individual as a grader for a
  period ending on December 31 of the following year and issue a
  certificate certifying that the individual is an accredited grader,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of a conformity verification
    body; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there is no conformity verification body, on
    payment of the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food
    Inspection Agency Fees Notice.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If the individual has obtained a mark of at least
  80% on the evaluation referred to in paragraph (1)(b) and at least 80%
  on the evaluation referred to in paragraph (1)(c), the Registrar shall
  issue to the individual a licence as a sampler for a period ending on
  December 31 of the following year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of a conformity verification
    body; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there is no conformity verification body, on
    payment of the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food
    Inspection Agency Fees Notice.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-183, s. 39\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless the accreditation of a grader or the licence
  of a sampler has been suspended or cancelled under section 13.2, and
  subject to subsection 13.2(7), the Registrar shall annually renew the
  accreditation or the licence, either on the recommendation of a
  conformity verification body or, if there is no conformity
  verification body, on payment of the applicable annual fee set out in
  the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice before January 1 of
  the year in respect of which the accreditation or licence is to be
  renewed.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The accreditation of a person as a grader or sampler
  is limited to those activities referred to in paragraphs (1)(b) to (d)
  for which the person has been evaluated and is qualified.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a conformity verification body refuses to make a
  recommendation to the Registrar that an applicant's accreditation as a
  grader or sampler be certified or renewed, the conformity verification
  body shall send a notice of that decision to the applicant by
  registered mail, giving the reasons for the decision and advising that
  the applicant may request that the Registrar review the decision. The
  conformity verification body shall also send a copy of the notice,
  including the reasons, to the Registrar.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The applicant may, within 30 days after the day on
  which the notice is received, make a written request to the Registrar
  for a review of the decision.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} The request shall include the reasons why the
  applicant believes that the Registrar should review the decision and
  may include or be accompanied by any documentation or information that
  the applicant considers appropriate. The request shall also include
  the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
  Notice.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the request, the Registrar shall
  review the decision.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} The review shall be conducted as informally and
  expeditiously as possible and in such a way as to permit the applicant
  to present the applicant's case and to reply to any evidence, but
  shall not include oral representations unless the Registrar determines
  that they are necessary.

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} If the Registrar finds that the conformity
  verification body should have recommended that the applicant's
  accreditation as a grader or sampler be certified or renewed, the
  Registrar shall certify the accreditation or renew it, as the case may
  be, as if the recommendation had been made.

- [(13)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall send a notice of the Registrar's
  decision and the reasons for it to the applicant by registered mail.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 39
- SOR/2001-93, s. 2
- SOR/2003-6, s. 107
- SOR/2007-223, s. 9
- SOR/2015-55, s. 12
:::

- **[[13.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  the Registrar shall suspend the accreditation of a grader or the
  licence of a sampler if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information has been submitted
    in support of the application for the accreditation or licence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grader or sampler does not comply with a
    provision of the Act or these Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not suspend the accreditation of
  a grader or the licence of a sampler if, before the grader or sampler
  is heard under paragraph (4)(b), the grader or sampler takes
  corrective measures and an inspector verifies that those measures have
  been taken.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) and (5), the Registrar
  shall cancel the accreditation of a grader or the licence of a sampler
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the grader or sampler does not pay the applicable
    annual fee before January 1 of the year in respect of which the
    accreditation or licence is to be renewed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a grader, the grader maintains any
    false or misleading records or samples in respect of any seed that
    the grader has graded, or the grader falsely represents any seed to
    be of pedigreed status;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sampler, the sampler maintains any
    false or misleading records or samples in respect of any seed that
    the sampler has sampled;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the grader or sampler provides false or misleading
    information to an inspector;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the accreditation of the grader or the licence of
    the sampler has been suspended three times within a 24-month period;
    or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the suspension of the accreditation or licence has
    been in effect for one year and the grader or sampler has not yet
    implemented corrective measures.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not suspend or cancel the
  accreditation of a grader or the licence of a sampler unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector has provided the grader or sampler
    with a written report setting out the reasons for the suspension or
    cancellation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has given the grader or sampler an
    opportunity to be heard, either by written or oral representations,
    in respect of the suspension or cancellation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has sent a notice of suspension or
    cancellation of the accreditation or licence to the grader or
    sampler.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not cancel the accreditation of a
  grader or the licence of a sampler for a reason set out in any of
  paragraphs (3)(a) to (d) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the grader or sampler establishes that the basis
    for the cancellation was the result of an error and the grader or
    sampler took precautions and exercised due diligence to prevent the
    occurrence of the error;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grader or sampler undertakes to notify persons
    likely to be affected by the error by placing an announcement in
    such media, and within such time, not exceeding 30 days, as the
    Registrar indicates; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the announcement
    referred to in paragraph (b) was made within the time indicated by
    the Registrar.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A suspension of an accreditation or licence remains
  in effect until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the grader or sampler
    has taken corrective measures; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar notifies the grader or sampler in
    writing that the suspension is lifted.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not renew the accreditation of a
  grader whose accreditation has been suspended three times, or the
  licence of a sampler whose licence has been suspended three times,
  unless after the third suspension the grader or sampler has
  successfully completed the applicable evaluations referred to in
  subsection 13.1(1).

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2007-223, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} If an individual's accreditation as a grader or
  licence as a sampler has been cancelled for a reason set out in any of
  paragraphs (3)(b) to (e), the Registrar shall not accept an
  application from the individual to be accredited or licensed again
  unless 24 months have gone by since the cancellation and the
  individual satisfies the conditions set out in subsections 13.1(1) to
  (2.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2001-93, s. 3
- SOR/2003-6, s. 108
- SOR/2007-223, s. 10
- [SOR/2018-108, s. 402](#1044660-1110063){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044660-1110063.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.2-20071025.html "Link to previous version of section 13.2"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2012-13,
s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Labelling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510622 .Subheading}

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 20(5), the
  labelling information that is required by these Regulations shall be
  shown conspicuously, legibly and indelibly in either one or both
  official languages.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No label shall show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any variation in the character, size, colour or
    placing of the printing that emphasizes or obscures any part of the
    labelling information required by these Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any incorrect or misleading information or mark; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any brand name or mark that might be construed as
    the name of a variety.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where any reference, direct or indirect, is made on a
  label to a place of production of the label or package and not to the
  place of production of the seed, the reference shall be accompanied by
  an additional statement indicating that the place of production refers
  only to the label or package.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where seed that is produced, packaged and labelled in
  a country other than Canada has applied to it a label that shows the
  identity and principal place of business of the person in Canada for
  whom the seed was produced for resale, the identity and principal
  place of business of that person shall be preceded by the words
  "imported by" or "imported for", unless the geographic origin of the
  seed is stated on the label.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 6](#1044452-1245021){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044452-1245021.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  units of measurement required to be shown on a label shall be the
  units of the International System of Units, in accordance with the
  [Weights and Measures Act](/eng/acts/W-6).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a unit of measurement is expressed in units of
  the International System of Units in accordance with subsection (1),
  other units of measurement may also be used.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 3
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  shall not be labelled with a grade name unless it has been

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} graded pursuant to section 13, in the case of seed
    to be labelled with a Canada pedigreed grade name; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tested in accordance with section 11, in all other
    cases.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every package of seed marked with the grade name
  "Canada Foundation Substandard", "Canada Foundation Substandard
  (Purity)", "Canada Registered Substandard" or "Canada Certified
  Substandard" shall indicate on a tag that is affixed to the package
  the percentage of germination of a representative sample of the seed
  determined pursuant to the appropriate test done in accordance with
  section 11 and the date on which the test was completed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every package of seed marked with a Varietal Blend
  grade name shall be labelled with the variety names and percentage of
  each variety.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-437, s. 3
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), every
  package of seed of a kind or species set out in Schedule I shall be
  labelled with the following information as determined pursuant to the
  appropriate test done in accordance with section 11:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and number of noxious weed seeds per unit
    weight;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and number of other weed seeds per unit
    weight or, where the standard set out in Schedule I is based on
    percentage by weight, the name and percentage by weight of other
    weed seeds;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and number of seeds of other crops per
    unit weight or, where the standard set out in Schedule I is based on
    percentage by weight, the name and percentage by weight of other
    crops;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of germination of a representative
    sample of the seed; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the test was completed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), every package of seed of a
  kind or species that is not set out in Schedule I shall be labelled
  with the information referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) as
  determined pursuant to the appropriate test done in accordance with
  section 11.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except for a package of seed marked with the grade
  name "Canada Foundation Substandard (Purity)", subsections (1) and 2
  do not apply where a vendor, in response to a request of a purchaser
  made within 1 year after the sale of a package, supplies the
  purchaser, in writing, within 30 days after the request is made, with
  the information specified in subsection (1) or (2), as the case may
  be.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 11(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  package containing 50 g or more of seed, other than tobacco seed,
  shall be marked with the net quantity of the seed contained in that
  package.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every package containing 25 g or more of tobacco seed
  shall be marked with the net quantity of the seed contained in that
  package.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 31, net quantity shall be declared
  by weight or by numerical count.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (3), corn that is labelled
  to indicate numerical count shall also be labelled to indicate net
  weight or number of seeds per unit weight.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every package containing more than 5 kg of seed shall
  be marked with the lot designation of the seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 2
- SOR/86-850, s. 8
- SOR/88-242, s. 4
- SOR/93-162, s. 5
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[pest control product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
  same meaning as in section 2 of the [Pest Control Products
  Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), any seed treated with a
  pest control product shall be thoroughly stained with a conspicuous
  colour to show that the seed has been so treated.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Seed that has been coated with any material that
  renders it conspicuous is exempt from the staining requirement.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where seed has been treated with a pest control
  product, the precautionary symbol and signal word prescribed by the
  regulations made under the [Pest Control Products
  Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01) to indicate the nature and degree of risk
  inherent in that product, together with the following statement, shall
  be marked on the package of the seed or on a conspicuous label
  attached to the package:

  Do not use for food or feed. This seed has been treated with ![blank
  line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-dot} (common or chemical name of
  pest control product)

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of seed that is sold in Canada and that
  has been treated with a pest control product, the precautionary symbol
  and signal word referred to in subsection (4) and the following
  statement shall be marked on the package of the seed, or on a
  conspicuous label attached to the package, in both official languages:

  "Do not use for food or feed. This seed has been treated with ![blank
  line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid} (common or chemical name of
  pest control product). [Ne pas utiliser pour l'alimentation des
  personnes ou des animaux. Cette semence a été traitée avec ![blank
  line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid} (nom commun ou chimique du
  produit antiparasitaire)."]{.otherLang lang="fr"}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-850, s. 9
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 7](#1044461-1245025){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044461-1245025.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Where the registration of
a variety is made subject to any of the terms and conditions set out in
section 68, the seed shall be labelled when sold to indicate that the
registration of the variety is restricted.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-850, s. 10
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Exemptions from Labelling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510676 .Subheading}

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Non-pedigreed seeds grown, sold and
  delivered by a producer on the producer's premises for seeding by a
  purchaser are exempt from the labelling requirements of sections 23 to
  31 if the producer does not advertise the seed for sale.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Seed may be imported into Canada without meeting the
  labelling requirements of section 15 to 19, 21 and 23 to 31, but must
  meet those requirements before it is subsequently sold.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Seeds of Field Crops]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510682 .Subheading}

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** Every package of seed of
the kinds or species set out in Tables I to II.1 and IV to VII to
Schedule I shall be labelled with the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
  labeller;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed, if applicable;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported seed corn, the name of the
  country of origin of production and, if from the United States, the
  state of origin of production;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed corn that is a mixture of two or
  more varieties, except for PPTM varietal blends, the name and
  percentage of each of the component varieties and either the
  approximate Corn Heat Unit rating or the designation "early", "medium"
  or "late", as appropriate;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hybrid seed of corn or hybrid seed of
  sunflower, sold or offered for sale as a Canada Foundation grade, the
  total number of seeds of other crops, per kilogram and the percentage
  of germination; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed that is labelled as oilseed rape,
  rapeseed or canola and that is not labelled with a Canada pedigreed
  grade name, the name "Polish type" or "[B. rapa]{.otherLang
  lang="la"}", "Argentine type" or "[B. napus]{.otherLang lang="la"}",
  "[B. juncea]{.otherLang lang="la"}", or the words "type not known", as
  appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-850, s. 12
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 12
- SOR/2012-13, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20071025.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Mixtures of Cereals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510694 .Subheading}

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  package of a mixture of cereal seeds of the kinds or species set out
  in Table III to Schedule I shall be labelled with the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
    labeller;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and percentage by weight of each kind or
    species of seed in the mixture; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of each variety of seed in the
    mixture, if applicable.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No Common No. 1 Cereal Mixture or Common No. 2 Cereal
  Mixture shall be labelled with a variety name.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-437, s. 5
- SOR/86-850, s. 13
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Forage Crop Seed]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510704 .Subheading}

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Every package of seed of
the kinds or species set out in Tables VIII to XII to Schedule I shall
be labelled with the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
  labeller;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed, if applicable;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of alfalfa and red clover seed that is
  imported, the country of origin of production and, if from the United
  States, the state of origin of production; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed that is red clover or of a
  mixture containing five per cent or more of red clover, the words
  "double-cut" or "single-cut", the letters "DC" or "SC", or the words
  "type not known", as appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-437, s. 6
- SOR/86-850, s. 14
- SOR/89-368, ss. 2(F), 4(F)
- SOR/91-609, s. 3
- SOR/93-162, s. 7
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Forage Seed Mixtures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510714 .Subheading}

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  package of a mixture of forage seeds of the kinds or species set out
  in Table XIII to Schedule I shall be labelled with the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
    labeller;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and percentage of each kind or species ---
    and of each variety, if applicable --- of seed that singly
    constitutes 3% or more by weight or, in the case of sweet clover, 1%
    or more by weight, of the mixture;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the percentage by weight of the kinds or species of
    seed of the mixture not stated on the label pursuant to
    paragraph (c) preceded by the words "other kinds"; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of alfalfa and red clover seed that is
    imported, the country of origin of production and, if from the
    United States, the state of origin of production, and the percentage
    included in the mixture.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding paragraph (1)(d), the label may show
  the name and percentage of each kind or species of seed in a package
  that singly constitutes one per cent or more by weight of the mixture.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information to be labelled on a package pursuant
  to subsections (1) and (2) shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the principal display panel of the package if
    the labelling information is on the package, or on one side of the
    tag if the labelling information is on the tag; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of a consistent size of type.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 5
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Lawn Grass and Turf Grass Seed]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510728 .Subheading}

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** Every package of seed of
the kinds or species set out in Tables XI and XII to Schedule I and
represented for use as lawn grass or turf grass shall be labelled with
the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
  labeller;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed, if applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 6
- SOR/82-898, s. 1
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Lawn Grass and Turf Grass Mixtures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510736 .Subheading}

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  package of seed that is a lawn grass mixture or turf grass mixture
  shall be labelled with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
    labeller;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the package contains more than 5 kg of seed, the
    name of each kind or species --- and of each variety, if applicable
    --- of seed that constitutes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} five per cent or more by weight of the mixture,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} three per cent or more by weight of the mixture,
      in the case of bentgrasses, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two per cent or more by weight of the mixture,
      in the case of white clover.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the name of any kind, species or variety of
  seed contained in a lawn grass mixture or turf grass mixture is stated
  on a label to the exclusion of the other kinds, species or varieties
  in the mixture, or in a manner to emphasize that name, the percentage
  by weight of that kind, species or variety in the mixture shall be
  stated on the label in the same size and colour of type as the name.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 7
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 14
- SOR/2012-286, s. 4(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20071025.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Ground Cover Mixtures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510748 .Subheading}

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  label shall not contain any words or designs to indicate or imply that
  seed that is a ground cover mixture is suitable for use on home lawns
  or for forage production.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every package of seed that is a ground cover mixture
  shall be labelled with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
    labeller;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of each kind or species of seed

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that constitutes five per cent or more by weight
      of the mixture, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that is listed in any provincial legislation as
      a noxious or restricted weed, where the seller, packager or
      labeller has the information referred to in subparagraph (i) and
      supplies the information in writing to purchasers on request.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the name of any kind, species or variety of
  seed contained in a ground cover mixture is stated on a label to the
  exclusion of the other kinds, species or varieties in the mixture, or
  in a manner to emphasize that name, the percentage by weight of that
  kind, species or variety in the mixture shall be stated on the label
  in the same size and colour of type as the name.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-367, s. 8
- SOR/85-903, s. 1
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Flowers, Herbs, Roots, Vegetables and Other Miscellaneous Seed including Onion Sets and Multiplier Onions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510760 .Subheading}

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** Every package of seed of
the kinds or species set out in Tables XVI to XXI to Schedule I and any
kinds or species not set out in Schedule I shall be labelled with the
following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
  labeller;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species --- and of the
  variety, if applicable --- of the seed, or in the case of mixtures,
  the name of each kind or species --- and of the varieties --- of the
  seed, if applicable;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the seed is sold or offered for sale on the basis
  of grade, the name of the grade of the particular seed; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a germination standard is prescribed for a
  particular kind or species under subsection 6(1) and the seed is not
  sold on the basis of a grade of a seed,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the year in which the seed was tested for
    germination pursuant to the appropriate test prescribed by section
    11 or the year for which the seed was packaged, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of germination of the seed or a
    minimum guaranteed percentage of germination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Specialty Seed]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510770 .Subheading}

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  seed that is specialty seed shall be labelled to indicate the kind or
  species of seed and the approximate percentage or net quantity of seed
  in the product.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every package of seed that is a specialty seed shall
  be labelled with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the seller, packager or
    labeller; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed, if applicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the labelling requirements referred to
  in subsection (2), every package of seed that is a specialty seed
  shall be labelled with the information required by the appropriate
  section of these Regulations with respect to that seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 17
- SOR/2015-55, s. 13(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20071025.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Seed Packaging and Official Tags]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510779 .Subheading}

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  official tag shall only be applied by or under the direction of an
  inspector or accredited grader.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except where seed is labelled with a Canada
  Substandard grade name, an official tag shall only contain the
  information required pursuant to sections 33 to 36.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3) and
  sections 35 and 37, every package of seed derived from a crop grown
  and conditioned in Canada and graded with a Canada pedigreed grade
  name shall be fastened and tagged with a domestic tag.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The domestic tag shall contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed, or in the case of a
    PPTM varietal blend, the names of the varieties in the blend;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of a mixture set out in Table
    III, XIII or XIV of Schedule I, the crop certificate number or, in
    the case of a blend of two or more original seed lots of certified
    status, whether of the same variety or a PPTM varietal blend, the
    two-digit seed year designation followed by the word "BLEND"; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the lot number.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An approved conditioner registered pursuant to Part
  IV is exempt from subsection (1) where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed is of certified status;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the package of seed has been fastened and labelled
    by that approved conditioner;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the seller, packager or labeller is
    marked on the package, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information required by subsection (2) is
      marked on the package and the layout of the information, the area
      marked and the colour conform to the domestic tag, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packages containing 2 kg or less
      of seed, the words "Certified Seed" appear in a blue rectangle
      that meets the colour requirements of the official tag and that
      has a length to width ratio of approximately 2:1 and the
      information set out in paragraphs (2)(a) to (c) and (e) appears
      elsewhere on the package; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packages referred to in subparagraph
    (c)(i), each time the approved conditioner proposes to obtain those
    packages, the approved conditioner notifies the Agency of that
    proposal.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The approved conditioner referred to in
  subsection (3) shall maintain complete and up-to-date records
  concerning the requests for and disposal of any package referred to in
  that subsection for a period of not less than two years following the
  disposal of the package.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2012-13, s. 5
:::
