- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (5)
  and sections 35 and 37, every package of seed derived in whole or in
  part from a crop not grown and conditioned in Canada and graded with a
  Canada pedigreed grade name shall be fastened and tagged with an
  inter-agency certification tag.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The inter-agency certification tag shall contain the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed or, in the case of a
    PPTM varietal blend, the names of the varieties in the blend;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the lot number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the state or country of the official certifying
    agency; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the pedigreed reference number of the official
    certifying agency.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An approved conditioner registered pursuant to Part
  IV is exempt from subsection (1) where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed is of certified status;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the package of seed has been fastened and labelled
    by that approved conditioner;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the seller, packager or labeller is
    marked on the package, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information required by subsection (2) is
      marked on the package and the layout of the information, the area
      marked and the colour conform to the inter-agency certification
      tag, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packages containing 2 kg or less
      of seed the words "Certified Seed" appear in a blue rectangle that
      meets the colour requirements of the official tag and that has a
      length to width ratio of approximately 2:1 and the information set
      out in paragraphs (2)(a) to (e) appears elsewhere on the package;
      and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of packages referred to in subparagraph
    (c)(i), each time the approved conditioner proposes to obtain those
    packages, the approved conditioner notifies the Agency of that
    proposal.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The approved conditioner referred to in
  subsection (3) shall maintain complete and up-to-date records
  concerning the requests for and disposal of any package referred to in
  that subsection for a period of not less than two years following the
  disposal of the package.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A package of seed of certified status is exempt from
  subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the package bears a label that was issued or
    approved by an official certifying agency and that indicates that
    the seed is of pedigreed status;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed labelled with a Canada
    pedigreed grade name in Canada, the Canada pedigreed grade name
    appears on a document that accompanies each sale of the seed and
    that bears the name of the person selling the seed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed imported into Canada labelled
    with a Canada pedigreed grade name, the Canada pedigreed grade name
    and the accredited grader number appear on a label attached to or
    printed directly on each package of seed.

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 37, every package
  of seed of a variety that is graded with a Canada pedigreed grade name
  shall be fastened and tagged with an unregistered variety tag if the
  seed is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of a kind, species or variety not exempt from
    registration pursuant to section 65;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of a variety not registered under Part III; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sold in Canada.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The unregistered variety tag shall contain the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the grade of the seed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the lot number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the seed is derived from a crop not grown in
    Canada, the country or the state of the official certifying agency
    and the pedigreed reference number of that agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 20
- SOR/2012-286, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20071025.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  pedigreed status tag may be applied to seed derived from a crop grown
  in Canada and not graded.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The pedigreed status tag shall contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the pedigreed status of the seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** Seed that has been graded
with a Canada pedigreed grade name is exempt from the requirement to be
sold in fastened packages where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed is sold in bulk and delivered by a bulk
  storage facility within the meaning of Part IV;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seed is accompanied by a completed official tag
  at the time of the sale;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} the seed, at the time of delivery, is accompanied
  by written information that, if the seed were in a fastened package,
  would be required by section 19, subsection 20(4) and sections 21 and
  35 to be marked on the package or to be contained on the package's
  label or tag; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vendor of the seed maintains complete and
  up-to-date records concerning the pedigree, conditioning, testing done
  in accordance with section 11 and disposal of the seed for a period of
  not less than one year following the disposal of the seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 21
:::

Version](section-37-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Advertising]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510856 .Subheading}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** Seed of any variety is
exempt from the operation of paragraph 3(1)(b) of the Act in so far as
it may be advertised for sale in Canada if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the variety has been reviewed by a recommending
  committee for registration pursuant to Part III;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an application for the registration of the variety
  has been received by the Registrar pursuant to Part III; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the advertising indicates that registration is
  pending.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
:::

### [Seed Crop Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510863 .Subheading}

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  crop inspection by inspectors of the Agency shall be limited to crops
  for the production of seed of pedigreed status and special seed crops.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The inspection of a seed crop shall be refused by an
  official of the Agency where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the request therefor is received too late in the
    season to make the inspection or to make arrangements for the
    inspection; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inspectors or facilities for inspection are not
    available.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 3
- SOR/86-850, s. 16(F)
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2000-184, s. 89
:::

### [Seed Importations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510871 .Subheading}

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (5) and (6),
  each seed lot that is imported into Canada shall be accompanied by a
  statement that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the kind or species of seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed for all kinds, species
    and varieties subject to registration under Part III, other than
    non-pedigreed seed of forage crops;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the lot designation of the seed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the exporter; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the importer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by subsection (5) or (6), the seed
  shall be accompanied by a seed analysis certificate indicating that
  the seed has been tested pursuant to paragraph 11(1)(b).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by subsection (5) or (6), the
  importer shall provide the Agency with a signed declaration stating

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information specified in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the country where the crop from which the seed is
    derived was grown;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the telephone number of the importer; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the intended purposes of the imported seed.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where seed is imported into Canada by a person other
  than an establishment registered pursuant to Part IV as an authorized
  importer, it shall be kept separate and intact in the original
  packages until a notice of release has been issued by the Agency
  indicating that the seed meets the requirements of these Regulations.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (4) do not apply in respect of a
  seed lot that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is composed of onion or garlic sets, tree seeds,
    shrub seeds, ginseng seeds, aquatic plant seeds, true potato seeds
    or flower seeds, except wildflower seed mixtures;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} weighs 5 kg or less, in the case of seed of the
    kinds or species set out in any of Tables I to VI and XVI to XVIII
    to Schedule I, or seeds of similar size or herb seeds; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} weighs 500 g or less, in the case of seed of the
    kinds or species set out in any of Tables VII to XII to Schedule I,
    or seeds of similar size.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply in respect of
  seed that is imported by an authorized importer where the authorized
  importer supplies the Agency with the establishment registration
  number at the time of importation and the information required by
  subsection (3) within 30 days after that time.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} If seed has been imported into Canada by an
  authorized importer under subsection (6), it shall be kept separate
  and intact in the original containers until a notice of release has
  been completed by an individual accredited under section 13.1 to
  evaluate imported seed and accompanying documents for conformity with
  these Regulations or the seed has been tested and found to be in
  conformity with these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2007-223, s. 22
:::

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), seed of
  any variety is exempt from the operation of paragraph 3(1)(b) of the
  Act if it is imported into Canada for the purpose of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conditioning;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} research;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} seeding by the importer; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sale pursuant to subsection 5(4).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Seed of any variety of spring wheat, winter wheat or
  durum wheat that is imported into the Canadian Wheat Board Area is
  exempt from the operation of paragraph 3(1)(b) of the Act only if it
  is imported for the purpose of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conditioning;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} research; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sale pursuant to subsection 5(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2008-228, s. 1
:::

### [Detentions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510908 .Subheading}

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  seed or package seized pursuant to section 8 of the Act may be
  detained by an inspector at any place by attaching a detention tag to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where only the seed is seized, the package provided
    by the Agency and in which the seed is placed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where only the package is seized, the package;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the package and the seed are seized, the
    package; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} where a seed lot in packages is seized, at least
    one package of the seed lot.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} On attaching a detention tag to the appropriate
  package referred to in subsection (1), the inspector shall deliver or
  mail a notice of the detention to the person entitled to possession,
  at the time of seizure, of the seed or package, as the case may be.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall alter or remove a detention tag
  attached to a package, or sell any seed or package detained, pursuant
  to subsection (1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall move any seed or package detained
  pursuant to subsection (1), except where an inspector issues a written
  authorization indicating that the seed or package must be placed in a
  safer or more convenient location.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless an official sample has already been taken of
  such seed, an inspector shall take an official sample of each seed lot
  that has been detained.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} On release from detention of the seed or package, an
  inspector shall deliver or mail a notice of release to the person
  entitled to possession, at the time of seizure, of the seed or
  package, as the case may be.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Costs incidental to the detention are payable and
  recoverable from the person entitled to possession, at the time of
  seizure, of the seed or package, as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/85-903, s. 3
- SOR/96-252, s. 2
- SOR/2000-184, s. 89
:::

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-534,
s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Seed Potatoes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-510924 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510925 .Subheading}

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Seeds
    Act](/eng/acts/S-8); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Food Inspection
    Agency established by section 3 of the [Canadian Food Inspection
    Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5); ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[aseptic environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aseptic environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility which
    excludes microorganisms that are capable of causing contamination or
    infection; ([milieu aseptique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bacterial ring rot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bacterial ring rot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the disease caused
    by the bacterial pathogen [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([flétrissement bactérien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Breeder's Selection seed potatoes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Breeder's Selection seed potatoes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means seed
    potatoes that are direct progeny of true seed, or of selected
    tubers, and that are grown for the purpose of evaluation as a
    potential variety for commercial use; ([pommes de terre de semences
    Choix du sélectionneur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[certificate number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[certificate number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the number that
    appears on a crop certificate to identify a crop and that appears on
    a seed potato tag, a record of bulk movement, a certificate issued
    under subsection 47.11(4), a permit issued under subsection 57(2) or
    a certificate of authorization issued under subsection 61(2) to
    identify the tubers produced by that crop; ([numéro de
    certificat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of seed potatoes
    established in section 47; ([classe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any piece of farming,
    storing, transporting or sorting equipment that is used jointly by
    two or more growers; ([équipement commun]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[crop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[crop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Breeder's Selection seed
    potatoes, or a variety and class of seed potatoes, growing in an
    aseptic environment, a protected environment or in one or more
    fields of a farm unit; ([culture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2000-184, s.
    84\]]{.Repealed}

[[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2000-184, s.
    84\]]{.Repealed}

[[farm unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[farm unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a single tract of land operated for the
      production and marketing of seed potatoes under the control of a
      grower, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a number of separate tracts of land operated as a
      single unit, with the use of common equipment, facilities or
      storage, for the production and marketing of seed potatoes under
      the control of the same grower; ([unité de
      production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[field]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[field]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the identifiable area of land on
    which Breeder's Selection seed potatoes, or seed potatoes of a
    particular variety and class, are planted or have been
    produced; ([champ]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[generation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[generation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2002-198, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[grower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a cooperative, a
    corporation or a partnership that grows seed
    potatoes; ([producteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[laboratory tests]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[laboratory tests]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means tests that are
    conducted in a laboratory that is accredited under the Agency
    Laboratory Accreditation Program or in a laboratory that can
    reasonably be considered to be equivalent to a laboratory accredited
    under that program; ([essais en laboratoire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the quantity of harvested seed
    potatoes of a variety and class that is identifiable by one
    certificate number or the quantity of Breeder's Selection seed
    potatoes that are identifiable by one certificate
    number; ([lot]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[non-certified potatoes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-certified potatoes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} potatoes that do not meet the standards for any
      of the classes established in section 47 or the standards
      established for Breeder's Selection seed potatoes in section 61.1,
      or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} seed potatoes that have been transported without
      seed potato tags, a record of bulk movement, a certificate issued
      under subsection 47.11(4), a permit issued under subsection 57(2)
      or a certificate of authorization issued under subsection
      61(2); ([pommes de terre non certifiées]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[nuclear stock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[nuclear stock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-118, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[protected environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[protected environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility for
    which there are appropriate procedures and physical barriers to
    prevent the entry of plant pathogens and insects; ([milieu
    protégé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[PSTV]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[PSTV]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the disease in a potato plant or
    tuber caused by the pathogen known as potato spindle tuber
    viroid; ([filosité des tubercules]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[record of bulk movement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[record of bulk movement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record
    provided to a grower under subsection 56(1); ([dossier de transport
    en vrac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[seed potato]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[seed potato]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tuber, or any part of a
    tuber, or nuclear stock, that is certified under these Regulations
    for seed-reproduction purposes; ([pomme de terre de
    semence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[seed potato tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[seed potato tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tag provided to a
    grower under subsection 53(1); ([étiquette de pommes de terre de
    semence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the movement of one lot, or
    any part thereof, of seed potatoes to a single
    consignee; ([expédition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[table stock potato]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[table stock potato]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2002-198, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[tuber unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tuber unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the separate pieces of one
    tuber that are planted consecutively in two or more hills in a
    row; ([tubercules individualisés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[variety]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[variety]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a variety of seed potatoes
    that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is distinguished by common morphological,
      physiological, cytological, chemical or other common
      characteristics, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} retains its distinguishing characteristics when
      reproduced; ([variété]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[volunteer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[volunteer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a growing plant that
    originates from a tuber that has been left in a field from a
    previous crop; ([plant spontané]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[zero tolerance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[zero tolerance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a disease, the requirement of the
      absence of the disease in a plant, or in any part thereof,
      including a tuber, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a varietal mixture, the requirement
      of the absence of the mixture of two or more
      varieties. ([tolérance zéro]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510967 .Subheading}

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of seed potatoes that are sold or advertised for sale in Canada,
imported into Canada or exported from Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 1
:::

**[[46.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2000-183, s. 40\]]{.Repealed}

### [Classes of Seed Potatoes and Class Names]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510972 .Subheading}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** The following classes of
seed potatoes are established, as listed, from the highest quality to
the lowest:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Nuclear Stock;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Pre-Elite;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Elite I;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Elite II;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Elite III;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Elite IV;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Foundation; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Certified.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 1
- SOR/97-118, s. 2
:::

### [Standards for Classes of Seed Potatoes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510984 .Subheading}

**[[47.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.1 .sectionLabel}** The standards for the
classes of seed potatoes established in section 47 are those set out in
sections 47.11 to 47.8.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 1
- SOR/97-118, s. 2
:::

### [Method of Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510988 .Subheading}

**[[47.101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.101 .sectionLabel}** The inspection of
seed potatoes shall be made either visually or through laboratory tests
or both.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-198, s. 2
:::

### [Nuclear Stock]{.HTitleText2} {#h-510992 .Subheading}

- **[[47.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nuclear Stock seed potatoes shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced from potato tissue culture material that
    has been tested and found free of bacterial ring rot, PSTV and
    viruses;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} laboratory tested within 12 months before the
    completion of the multiplication process and found free of bacterial
    ring rot, PSTV and viruses;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} produced in an aseptic or protected environment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} visibly free from varietal mixtures;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} free from pathogenic bacteria or viruses,
    saprophytic contamination or other symptoms of diseases that could
    affect the quality of the material; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if produced in a protected environment,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} inspected by an inspector at least once during
      the growing period, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} grown in a medium that has not been previously
      used.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph 6(1)(d) of the Act, an inspector
  may ask a grower to provide records that contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the origin of each variety;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the testing history of each variety;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the pedigree of each variety; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence that subparagraph (1)(f)(ii) has been
    complied with.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any material that is being produced as Nuclear Stock
  seed potatoes shall be removed without delay from an aseptic or
  protected environment if it tests positive for pathogenic bacterial or
  viral organisms or manifests signs or symptoms of saprophytic
  contamination.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who sells or transfers Nuclear Stock seed
  potatoes shall identify those seed potatoes with a seed potato tag or
  a certificate provided by an inspector that indicates that the seed
  potatoes are Nuclear Stock seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-118, s. 2
- SOR/97-292, s. 35
- SOR/2000-184, s. 91
- SOR/2002-198, s. 3
- SOR/2017-94, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47.11-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47.11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Pre-Elite]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511012 .Subheading}

- **[[47.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pre-Elite seed potatoes shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced from Nuclear Stock seed potatoes, from
    cuttings or plants that were produced in a protected environment or
    from tubers or selected clones, that were determined by laboratory
    tests to be free from any disease that could affect the quality of
    the seed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} produced in a field where potatoes were not planted
    for the previous two years; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} inspected by an inspector at least two times during
    the growing season.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Pre-Elite seed potatoes shall be given the status of
  field generation 1 if the crop is produced in the field for the first
  time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of plants showing visible varietal
  mixture or visible symptoms of disease set out in column 1 of an item
  of the table to this section on the first inspection, any subsequent
  inspection and the final inspection shall not exceed the applicable
  percentages set out in columns 2 and 3 of that item.

### [Elite I]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511029 .Subheading}

- **[[47.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Elite I seed potatoes shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced from Nuclear Stock or Pre-Elite seed
    potatoes; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inspected by an inspector at least two times during
    the growing season.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Elite I seed potatoes shall be given the status of
  field generation 2 if the crop is produced in the field for the second
  time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of plants showing visible varietal
  mixture or visible symptoms of disease set out in column 1 of an item
  of the table to this section on the first inspection, any subsequent
  inspection and the final inspection shall not exceed the applicable
  percentages set out in columns 2 and 3 of that item.

  <td class="noBorderRight top">0.4</td>
  <td class="top">0.2</td>

### [Certified]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511109 .Subheading}

- **[[47.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Certified seed potatoes shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced from Foundation or a higher class of seed
    potatoes; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inspected by an inspector at least two times during
    the growing season.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Certified seed potatoes shall be given the status of
  field generation 7 if the crop is produced in the field for the
  seventh time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of plants showing visible varietal
  mixture or visible symptoms of disease set out in column 1 of an item
  of the table to this section on the first inspection, any subsequent
  inspection and the final inspection shall not exceed the applicable
  percentages set out in columns 2 and 3 of that item.

### [Tuber Grade Sizes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511125 .Subheading}

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  documentation for any quantity of graded seed potatoes, whether in
  containers or in bulk, shall specify, in millimetres, the minimum and
  maximum size of the graded tubers.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the minimum size
  specified shall not be less than 30 mm and the maximum size specified
  shall not be greater than 70 mm for long type varieties or 80 mm for
  round type varieties.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to graded seed potatoes
  that are sold under an agreement that states the quantity of graded
  seed potatoes to be purchased and in which the grower or supplier and
  the purchaser have agreed to a different minimum and maximum size than
  the ones set out in that subsection.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any quantity of seed potatoes, whether supplied in
  containers or in bulk, shall be sorted and graded by the grower or
  supplier and at least 95% of the tubers by weight shall fall within
  the minimum and maximum sizes referred to in subsection (2) or (3), as
  applicable.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply to Nuclear Stock seed
  potatoes or Breeder's Selection seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-198, s. 3
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 6](#1515076-1516110){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515076-1516110.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Standards for Tubers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511138 .Subheading}

- **[[48.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes of a class established
  in section 47 shall meet the standards prescribed in subsection (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of tubers in any lot showing symptoms
  of any disease or defect set out in Column I of an item of the table
  to this subsection shall not, at the point of shipping, exceed the
  percentage set out in Column II of that item and shall not, at
  destination, exceed the percentage set out in Column III of that item.

  <figure id="511142">

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the table to subsection (2),

  [[light Rhizoctonia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light Rhizoctonia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that 1 to 5 per
      cent of the tuber surface is covered with Rhizoctonia sclerotia;
      ([rhizoctonie légère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[light scab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light scab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that 1 to 5 per cent of
      the tuber surface is covered with common scab lesions; ([gale
      légère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[moderate Rhizoctonia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[moderate Rhizoctonia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that 5 to 10
      per cent of the tuber surface is covered with Rhizoctonia
      sclerotia; ([rhizoctonie modérée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[moderate scab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[moderate scab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that 5 to 10 per cent
      of the tuber surface is covered with common scab lesions. ([gale
      modérée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In any lot, the number of tubers that are affected by
  light and moderate scab and Rhizoctonia combined shall not exceed 10
  per cent of the total number of tubers in the lot.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2002-198, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The percentage of tubers found in a lot of any class
  that is of a variety other than the variety of which the lot generally
  consists shall not exceed the percentage permitted by sections 47.11
  to 47.8 for that class.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} In any lot, the number of tubers in the aggregate
  that are affected by disease and defects, not including light scab,
  light Rhizoctonia and stem-end discolouration, shall not exceed 5 per
  cent of the total number of tubers in the lot.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} At the point of shipping, not more than 5 per cent of
  tubers in a lot shall be affected by pressure bruising on over 10 per
  cent of their surface.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} At the point of shipping, not more than 10 per cent
  of the tubers in a lot shall have sprouts longer than 2 cm.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} In any lot, at least 98 per cent of tubers shall be
  firm and well shaped.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} No tubers in any lot shall be washed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 1
- SOR/2002-198, s. 4
:::

### [Application for Crop Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511164 .Subheading}

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application by a grower for an inspection of a crop shall be made on a
  form provided by the Agency and shall include the following
  information and be accompanied by the following documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} seed potato tags or other documents that attest to
    the origin and class of each seed lot planted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a list of all of the grower's crops;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a crop that was planted with seed
    potatoes that were produced by the grower, information or documents
    that show that at least two field-grown lots from the grower's farm
    unit have been subjected to laboratory tests and not found positive
    for the presence of [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"};

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a crop that was planted with Elite
    II, Elite III, Elite IV or Foundation seed potatoes that were not
    produced by the grower, information or documents that show that
    those seed potatoes have been subjected to laboratory tests and not
    found positive for [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"}
    subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application made by a new seed
    potato grower, a document, issued by an inspector, that verifies
    that a clean-up and disinfection of the premises at the grower's
    farm unit was completed before the arrival of the seed potatoes for
    planting and that the crop was not planted in a field where
    non-certified potatoes were grown in either of the previous two
    years.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application shall be delivered to the office of
  the Agency specified on the form by the following deadlines:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of field-grown crops, on or before June
    30 of the crop year in respect of which the application is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of crops produced in a protected
    environment, 30 days after planting; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of crops produced in an aseptic
    environment, before material is transferred to a new owner of the
    material or transferred to a protected environment.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application may be made after the deadline
  specified in subsection (2) if there is still time for the inspection
  to be carried out in accordance with these Regulations and the grower
  shows that the delay in submitting the application was beyond the
  grower's control.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 1
- SOR/95-215, s. 2
- SOR/97-118, s. 7
- SOR/97-292, s. 36
- SOR/2000-183, s. 41
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
- SOR/2002-198, s. 5
- SOR/2017-94, s. 10(F)
:::

### [Conditions for Crop Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511179 .Subheading}

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4),
  inspection of a crop shall be made if the crop is planted with seed
  potatoes that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are of a variety that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is registered in accordance with Part III, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is not registered, if the potatoes are being
      grown solely for experimental purposes or for evaluation for the
      purposes of registration; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are of a class established in section 47 or are
    Breeder's Selection seed potatoes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall inspect a crop that is planted
  with imported seed potatoes if the seed potatoes meet the requirements
  of paragraphs 59(2)(a) to (c).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not inspect a crop of a grower if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the current year,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} non-certified potatoes or Certified seed potatoes
      have been planted in fields under cultivation by the grower,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the crop is growing in a field where
      non-certified potatoes were planted in the previous two years, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the crop is growing in a field where there has
      been an occurrence of bacterial ring rot, unless the inspector
      verifies that the field has been free of potatoes, including
      volunteers, for the previous two years; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the previous year, potatoes infected with
    [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"} were produced in fields under
    cultivation by the grower, or were found in buildings or on
    equipment used by the grower and a clean-up and disinfection of the
    building and equipment was not verified by an inspector.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not inspect a crop of a grower if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a result of late planting, lack of cultivation,
    lack of vigour, the existence of weeds, leaf injury or pesticide
    injury, an inspector is unable to conduct a visual inspection of the
    crop;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the distance between adjacent fields is less than
    the space required for one blank row;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} there are not at least 10 m of blank row at each
    end of the field to separate one class of a variety from another
    class of the same variety; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} equipment used in relation to planting, cultivating
    or spraying the crop has been exposed to contamination by pathogens
    that are or may be detrimental to the crop, unless the equipment,
    each time before it has entered a field of a farm unit, has been
    thoroughly cleaned and disinfected in such a way as to destroy the
    contamination and the grower demonstrates to the inspector that the
    clean-up and disinfection has been completed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-517, s. 1
- SOR/86-849, s. 6
- SOR/91-526, s. 2
- SOR/93-331, s. 2
- SOR/95-179, s. 10
- SOR/97-118, ss. 8, 18
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
- SOR/2002-198, s. 6
:::

### [Crop Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511200 .Subheading}

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At the
  time of inspection of a crop, an inspector shall inspect the crop to
  determine whether it meets the applicable standards stated in sections
  47.11 to 47.8 and 61.1.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before the first inspection of the crop, the grower
  shall submit for examination by an inspector all seed potato tags and
  other documents pertaining to the source of the seed potatoes planted
  on the farm unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/97-118, s. 9
- SOR/2002-198, s. 7
:::

### [Crop Certificate]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511206 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4),
  if an inspector has inspected crops on a farm unit and finds that they
  meet the applicable standards set out in sections 47.11 to 47.8 and
  61.1, the inspector shall issue to the grower a crop certificate.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of Breeder's Selection seed potatoes,
  the crop certificate shall specify for each crop

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information by which the crop is identified on
    the application made under section 49;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of plants or the number of hectares that
    passed inspection; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the certificate number.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of seed potatoes of a class and
  variety, the crop certificate shall specify for each crop

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the class and variety of the seed potatoes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of plants or the number of hectares that
    passed inspection; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the certificate number.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not issue a crop certificate in
  respect of a particular crop where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the crop was not top-killed as directed by an
    inspector;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the crop has been treated with a sprout inhibitor;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the inspector determines that the crop has been
    exposed to a sprout inhibitor from any source.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not issue a crop certificate in
  respect of any crop of a grower if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inspector determines, based on crop inspection
    and laboratory verification, that any crop under production by the
    grower is infected with [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any common or custom equipment was used in cutting
    tubers infected with [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}, or in
    planting, spraying or cultivating a crop infected with [Clavibacter
    michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} unless the equipment, each time prior to entering a field
    of a farm unit, has been thoroughly cleaned and disinfected in such
    a way as to destroy the contamination and the grower demonstrates to
    the inspector that the clean-up and disinfection has been completed.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not issue a crop certificate in
  respect of a crop if the inspector determines that the crop has been
  infected with the potato spindle tuber viroid or that pathogen was
  present in the source of seed potatoes used to plant that crop.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall revoke a crop certificate issued
  pursuant to subsection (1) in respect of a particular crop at any time
  if the inspector determines that the corresponding lot

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been exposed to sprout inhibitors or other
    harmful chemicals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has lost its identity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has lost its ability to propagate;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is infested with a pest as defined in section 3 of
    the [Plant Protection Act](/eng/acts/P-14.8); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is infected with potato spindle tuber viroid or was
    produced from a source of seed potatoes infected with that pathogen.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall revoke all crop certificates
  issued pursuant to subsection (1) in respect of the crops of a farm
  unit where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inspector determines that any one of the lots
    is infected with [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"}
    subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any one of the lots came in contact with any common
    or custom equipment used on a potato operation that was infected
    with [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"} unless the inspector has
    verified that a clean-up and disinfection of the equipment had been
    completed before the use of the equipment on the crops.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall revoke a crop certificate issued
  pursuant to subsection (1) where the inspector determines that
  non-certified potatoes from another farm unit are stored with the
  certified seed potatoes or that the certified seed potatoes were
  stored, graded or handled with common equipment.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} After a crop certificate has been issued, an
  inspector shall, if requested by a certification authority, issue a
  North American Certified Seed Potato Health Certificate to
  substantiate the health status of a seed potato lot to which the crop
  certificate applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-517, s. 2
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/95-179, s. 11
- SOR/97-118, ss. 10, 18
- SOR/2002-198, s. 8
:::

### [Tuber Storage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511238 .Subheading}

- **[[52.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  grower shall store each lot in a manner that prevents varietal mixture
  and mixture with non-certified potatoes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where lots of different classes of a single variety
  are stored in the same bin without separation to prevent mixture, the
  seed potatoes shall be given the certificate number of the lot with
  the lowest class.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where two or more lots of the same class of a single
  variety are stored in the same bin without separation to prevent
  mixture, the seed potatoes shall be given the certificate number of
  the lot with the highest disease percentage.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A grower shall identify each lot in storage according
  to the name of the applicable class established in section 47 or the
  name "Breeder's Selection", as the case may be.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A grower shall hold in storage any lot, or any part
  of any lot, that may have been exposed to freezing temperatures or
  that may be infected with late blight, [Fusarium]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} or other tuber-rotting organisms, until such time has
  passed during which any defects or symptoms could reasonably be
  expected to develop, so that an inspector is able to determine whether
  the lot meets the standards set out in subsections 48.1(2) to (10).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/2002-198, s. 9
:::

### [Seed Potato Tags]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511247 .Subheading}

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), an
  inspector shall provide seed potato tags to a grower for use by the
  grower in labelling seed potatoes, other than Breeder's Selection seed
  potatoes, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a request from the grower has been received by the
    inspector in respect of a crop for which a crop certificate was
    issued; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop for which the
    seed potato tags are provided, except for Pre-Elite, Elite I and
    Certified classes, has been subjected to laboratory tests and not
    found positive for the presence of [Clavibacter
    michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A grower who receives seed potato tags shall grade
  seed potatoes in accordance with section 48 and subsections 48.1(2) to
  (10).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A grower who receives seed potato tags shall attach
  them to the containers of seed potatoes that were produced from a crop
  in respect of which a crop certificate has been issued.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an inspector, on inspection, determines that seed
  potatoes for which seed potato tags were provided under subsection (1)
  do not meet the standards or grade size set out in sections 47.11 to
  47.8, 48 and 48.1, the inspector shall request the grower to re-grade
  the seed potatoes, and if the grower refuses to re-grade or cannot
  re-grade the seed potatoes, the grower shall return the seed potato
  tags to the inspector.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every seed potato tag shall have printed on it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the class, variety and size of the seed potatoes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any additional information that is necessary to
    meet the requirements of the laws of the country that will be
    importing the seed potatoes;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the information set out in
    paragraphs (a) to (c) is printed on the seed potato tag; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the following statements, in French and English:

    > ::: QuotedText
    > THE GROWER declares that the seed potatoes contained in this
    > shipment are from the crop for which a crop certificate was issued
    > bearing the crop certificate number shown on this tag and that
    > they are graded in accordance with section 48 and subsections
    > 48.1(2) to (10) of the [Seeds
    > Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1400).
    >
    > A request for reinspection of the seed potato lot may be made to
    > the Canadian Food Inspection Agency within two working days after
    > receipt of the lot.
    >
    > NOTE
    >
    > Inspection for the purpose of certification was made either
    > visually or through laboratory testing of samples, or both.
    > Certification does not constitute a warranty, by either the
    > Canadian Food Inspection Agency or the grower, that the seed
    > potatoes meet the applicable standards set out in sections 47.11
    > to 47.8 of the [Seeds
    > Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1400).
    > :::

    > ::: QuotedText
    > [LE PRODUCTEUR déclare que les pommes de terre de semence qui font
    > l'objet de la présente expédition proviennent de la culture
    > certifiée par le certificat de culture dont le numéro figure sur
    > la présente étiquette et qu'elles ont été classées conformément à
    > l'article 48 et aux paragraphes 48.1(2) à (10) du [Règlement sur
    > les semences](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_ch._1400).]{.otherLang
    > lang="fr"}
    >
    > [Une demande de réinspection du lot de pommes de terre de semence
    > peut être faite à l'Agence canadienne d'inspection des aliments
    > dans les deux jours ouvrables suivant la réception du
    > lot.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    >
    > [REMARQUE]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    >
    > [L'inspection en vue de la certification a été effectuée soit
    > visuellement, soit au moyen d'essais de laboratoire sur des
    > échantillons, soit par les deux moyens. La certification ne
    > constitue pas une garantie, de la part de l'Agence canadienne
    > d'inspection des aliments ou du producteur, que les pommes de
    > terre de semence satisfont aux normes applicables énoncées aux
    > articles 47.11 à 47.8 du [Règlement sur les
    > semences](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_ch._1400).]{.otherLang
    > lang="fr"}
    > :::

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not provide seed potato tags to a
  grower in respect of a lot of Pre-Elite, Elite I or Elite II class of
  seed potatoes if the grower was not granted a crop certificate in each
  of the previous three years due to the presence of [Clavibacter
  michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} in a field, in storage or on equipment of that grower.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/95-179, s. 12
- SOR/97-118, ss. 11, 18
- SOR/97-292, s. 37
- SOR/2002-198, s. 10
:::

### [Packaging and Marking]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511264 .Subheading}

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3) and
  section 56, seed potatoes shall be packaged in containers that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contain not less than 20 kg;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are new;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} are closed after packaging; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have no markings that refer to non-certified
    potatoes or are likely to result in the seed potatoes being mistaken
    as such.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A seed potato tag shall be attached to each container
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes other than Nuclear Stock seed potatoes
  may be packaged in containers that contain less than 20 kg if an
  inspector issues a permit to that effect.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A permit referred to in subsection (3) shall be
  issued by an inspector on the request of the packager and shall be
  valid for a period of one year.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Nuclear Stock seed potatoes may be packaged in
  containers that contain less than 20 kg if an inspector's certificate
  certifying that the seed potatoes are Nuclear Stock seed potatoes or a
  seed potato tag has been provided in respect of the seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/95-179, s. 13
- SOR/2002-198, s. 11
:::

### [Repackaging]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511277 .Subheading}

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  potatoes, other than Nuclear Stock seed potatoes, that were packaged
  in accordance with section 54 may be repackaged if the containers used
  for repackaging are new and, in the case of containers that contain
  less than 20 kg, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector has issued a permit, on request of the
    packager, valid for a period of one year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the container is
    labelled to show

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the packager,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the size of the seed potatoes and, in the case
      of seed potatoes other than Breeder's Selection seed potatoes, the
      class and variety of the seed potatoes, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed potatoes other than Breeder's
    Selection seed potatoes, the person repackaging the seed potatoes
    returns to the inspector who has issued the permit referred to in
    paragraph (a) the seed potato tags that were attached to the
    original containers or the record of bulk movement that accompanied
    the original shipment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes that are imported from the United
  States for the purposes of repackaging shall be repackaged in new
  containers and labelled by the packager to show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the packager;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the variety of the seed potatoes;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the state in which the seed potatoes
    were grown; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/2002-198, s. 12
:::

### [Record of Bulk Movement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511293 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2002-198, s. 13

\]
:::

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (9), an
  inspector shall provide a record of bulk movement to a grower in
  respect of seed potatoes, other than Breeder's Selection seed
  potatoes, before they are shipped in bulk if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a request from the grower has been received by the
    inspector in respect of a crop for which a crop certificate was
    issued; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop for which the
    record of bulk movement is to be provided, except for Pre-Elite,
    Elite I and Certified classes, has been subjected to laboratory
    tests and not found positive for the presence of [Clavibacter
    michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A grower who receives a record of bulk movement shall
  grade the seed potatoes referred to in subsection (1) in accordance
  with section 48 and subsections 48.1(2) to (10).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A grower who receives a record of bulk movement shall
  clean and disinfect the equipment to be used in the handling and
  shipping in bulk of the seed potatoes before that handling and
  shipping.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No record of bulk movement that accompanies a bulk
  shipment of seed potatoes is valid unless it has written on it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the weight, class, variety and size of the seed
    potatoes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the crop certificate number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the consignee;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any additional information that is necessary to
    meet the requirements of the laws of the country that will be
    importing the seed potatoes;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the information set out in
    paragraphs (a) to (d) is printed on the record of bulk movement; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the following statements, in French and English:

    > ::: QuotedText
    > THE GROWER declares that the seed potatoes contained in this
    > shipment are from the crop for which a crop certificate was issued
    > bearing the crop certificate number shown on this record of bulk
    > movement and that they are graded in accordance with section 48
    > and subsections 48.1(2) to (10) of the [Seeds
    > Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1400).
    >
    > THE GROWER declares that the vehicle carrying this shipment was
    > disinfected, before loading, with a control product registered
    > under the [Pest Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01) for use
    > against [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    > [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}.
    >
    > A request for reinspection of the seed potato lot may be made to
    > the Canadian Food Inspection Agency within two working days after
    > receipt of the lot.
    >
    > NOTE
    >
    > Inspection for the purpose of certification was made either
    > visually or through laboratory testing of samples, or both.
    > Certification does not constitute a warranty, by either the
    > Canadian Food Inspection Agency or the grower, that the seed
    > potatoes meet the applicable standards set out in sections 47.11
    > to 47.8 of the [Seeds
    > Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1400).
    > :::

    > ::: QuotedText
    > [LE PRODUCTEUR déclare que les pommes de terre de semence qui font
    > l'objet de la présente expédition proviennent de la culture
    > certifiée par le certificat de culture dont le numéro figure sur
    > le présent dossier de transport en vrac et qu'elles ont été
    > classées conformément à l'article 48 et aux paragraphes 48.1(2)
    > à (10) du [Règlement sur les
    > semences](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_ch._1400).]{.otherLang
    > lang="fr"}
    >
    > [LE PRODUCTEUR déclare que le véhicule servant au transport de
    > cette expédition a été désinfecté, avant le chargement, avec un
    > produit antiparasitaire homologué conformément à la [Loi sur les
    > produits antiparasitaires](/eng/acts/P-9.01) pour la lutte contre
    > la [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    > [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    >
    > [Une demande de réinspection du lot de pommes de terre de semence
    > peut être faite à l'Agence canadienne d'inspection des aliments
    > dans les deux jours ouvrables suivant la réception du
    > lot.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    >
    > [REMARQUE]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    >
    > [L'inspection en vue de la certification a été effectuée soit
    > visuellement, soit au moyen d'essais de laboratoire sur des
    > échantillons, soit par les deux moyens. La certification ne
    > constitue pas une garantie, de la part de l'Agence canadienne
    > d'inspection des aliments ou du producteur, que les pommes de
    > terre de semence satisfont aux normes applicables énoncées aux
    > articles 47.11 à 47.8 du [Règlement sur les semences]{.otherLang
    > lang="la"}.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}
    > :::

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A record of bulk movement is not transferable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes that are sold in bulk for the purposes
  of packaging and resale are not required to be graded in accordance
  with section 48 and subsections 48.1(2) to (10) where the grower has
  received a permit to that effect from an inspector before the sale.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The permit referred to in subsection (6) shall be
  issued by an inspector at the request of the grower after a sample of
  the lot or growing crop, except for Pre-Elite, Elite I and Certified
  classes, has been subjected to laboratory tests and not found positive
  for the presence of [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"}.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} At the time of packaging, seed potatoes referred to
  in subsection (6) shall be graded in accordance with section 48 and
  subsections 48.1(2) to (10).

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} An inspector shall not provide a record of bulk
  movement to a grower in respect of a lot of Pre-Elite, Elite I or
  Elite II class of seed potatoes if the grower was not granted a crop
  certificate in each of the previous three years due to the presence of
  [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
  [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"} in a field, in storage or on
  equipment of that grower.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-661, s. 3
- SOR/85-903, s. 6
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/95-179, s. 14
- SOR/97-118, ss. 12, 18
- SOR/97-292, s. 38
- SOR/2002-198, s. 14
:::

### [Tuber Damage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511316 .Subheading}

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  potatoes of any class that do not meet the standards set out in
  subsections 48.1(2) to (10) due to the existence of one defect of a
  mechanical or physiological nature may be sold in Canada where the
  President has issued a permit to the grower to that effect.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The President shall issue a permit referred to in
  subsection (1) where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is a shortage of seed potatoes of the
    particular variety and class in Canada that meet the applicable
    standards set out in sections 47.11 to 47.8;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop in respect of
    which the permit is to be issued, except for Pre-Elite, Elite I and
    Certified classes, has been subjected to laboratory tests and not
    found positive for the presence of [Clavibacter
    michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"};

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the seed potatoes will be used by the purchaser
    solely for the purchaser's own planting; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} before the sale of the seed potatoes, the purchaser
    provides the President with a statement, signed by the grower and
    the consignee, that specifies the variety, class and certificate
    number of the seed potatoes and the nature of the defect.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A copy of the permit referred to in subsection (1)
  shall accompany each shipment.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a bulk-movement of seed potatoes
  referred to in subsection (1), the grower shall have the equipment
  that is to be used to transport the potatoes cleaned and disinfected
  before loading.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/95-179, s. 15
- SOR/97-118, s. 13
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
:::

### [Potato Eyes and Cut-Seed Pieces]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511328 .Subheading}

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Potato
  eyes and cut-seed pieces may be certified as seed potatoes where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector determines that the tubers, before
    cutting, meet the standards set out in section 48, subsections
    48.1(2) to (10) and in subsection (2) of this section;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they are cut from seed potatoes of a class
    established in section 47;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person who performed the cutting is authorized
    to do so by a permit issued annually by the President; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop from which the
    potato eyes and cut-seed pieces originate, except for Pre-Elite,
    Elite I or Certified classes, has been subjected to laboratory tests
    and not found positive for the presence of [Clavibacter
    michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp. [sepedonicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} All potato eyes and cut-seed pieces shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have an average mass of at least 15 g at the time
    they are cut; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be firm and free of visible damage from bacteria,
    fungi or insects.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The President shall issue a permit referred to in
  paragraph (1)(c) where the person referred to in that paragraph
  submits a declaration stating that the person will maintain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the lot identity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sanitary conditions at the cutting facility.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every container of potato eyes or cut-seed pieces
  shall be labelled by the packager to show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the packager; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the class, variety and crop certificate number of
    the lot from which they were cut.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Cut-seed pieces may be transported in bulk if they
  are accompanied by a record of bulk movement.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} At least 95 per cent of the potato eyes or cut-seed
  pieces in any shipment shall have a depth of at least 20 mm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/97-118, s. 14
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
- SOR/2002-198, s. 15
:::

### [Importation for Certification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511348 .Subheading}

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Seed
  potatoes of a variety that has been registered under Part III may be
  imported for certification if prior authorization to do so has been
  obtained from the President.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The President shall give an authorization referred to
  in subsection (1) where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed potatoes have been certified in the
    country from which they are imported and packaged in accordance with
    the requirements of the recognized certification authority of that
    country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the certification and packaging requirements of the
    agency referred to in paragraph (a) are substantively equivalent to
    those contained in these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot has been subjected to
    laboratory tests and not found positive for the presence of
    [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"} and PSTV.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes that have not been registered under
  Part III may be imported if prior authorization to do so has been
  obtained from the President.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The President shall give an authorization referred to
  in subsection (3) where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seed potatoes are to be planted by the importer
    for experimental purposes or for production for export; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of paragraphs (2)(a) to (c) have
    been met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 7
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/97-118, s. 15
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
- SOR/2002-198, s. 16
:::

### [Detention]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511361 .Subheading}

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  seed potatoes that are seized pursuant to the Act are detained, an
  inspector shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} attach a detention tag to at least one of the
    containers of the seed potatoes or, where the seed potatoes are in
    bulk, place a detention tag where the potatoes are located; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} deliver or mail to the person having the care or
    control of the seed potatoes a notice of the detention that
    describes the seed potatoes and the place where they are being
    detained.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The detention tag referred to in subsection (1) shall
  be in a form established by the President and shall set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the seed potatoes seized and
    detained;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the seizure and detention;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the seizure and detention;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and signature of the inspector;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the telephone number to call for further
    information in respect of the seizure and detention; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} such other information as is necessary to identify
    the seed potatoes.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except where an inspector provides written
  authorization for a change in location of detained seed potatoes, no
  person shall remove seed potatoes from the place where they are being
  detained.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall alter or remove a detention tag that
  is attached to a container or bulk shipment of seed potatoes.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where an inspector determines that any seized and
  detained seed potatoes meet the requirements of the Act and these
  Regulations, the inspector shall release the seed potatoes and shall
  deliver or mail a copy of the release document to the person referred
  to in paragraph (1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/97-118, s. 16
- SOR/2000-184, s. 91
:::

### [Reinspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511378 .Subheading}

- **[[60.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector may reinspect seed potatoes at any time, and shall place
  under detention any seed potatoes that were not graded in accordance
  with section 48 or that do not meet the standards set out in
  subsections 48.1(2) to (10).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request for reinspection of a lot may be made
  within two working days after receipt of the lot, and the reinspection
  shall be carried out by an inspector as soon as possible but no later
  than five days after receipt of the request.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Seed potatoes placed under detention pursuant to
  subsection (1) may be re-graded in accordance with section 48 and
  subsections 48.1(2) to (10).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If seed potatoes are not graded in accordance with
  section 48 and do not meet the standards set out in subsections
  48.1(2) to (10), the seed potato tags or record of bulk movement that
  is provided with respect to those potatoes shall be returned to the
  inspector.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/2002-198, s. 17
- SOR/2017-94, s. 11(F)
:::

### [Shipment of Breeder's Selection Seed Potatoes or Non-Registered Varieties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511386 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2002-198, s. 18

\]
:::

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who ships Breeder's Selection seed potatoes or seed potatoes of
  a non-registered variety between farm units within Canada shall
  include with the shipment a copy of the certificate of authorization
  that is issued by the President under subsection (2) and that
  identifies the seed potatoes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The President shall issue a certificate of
  authorization if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the lot is to be grown for experimental purposes or
    for multiplication for the purposes of registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop, except for
    Pre-Elite, Elite I and Certified classes, has been subjected to
    laboratory tests and not found positive for the presence of
    [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    [sepedonicus;]{.otherLang lang="la"}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in sections 48 and 48.1
    have been met in relation to the seed potatoes; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a crop certificate has been issued for the seed
    potatoes.

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2025-47, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/97-118, s. 17
- SOR/2000-184, s. 90
- SOR/2002-198, s. 19
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 7](#1515078-1516116){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515078-1516116.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-61-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
