### [Breeder's Selection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511399 .Subheading}

- **[[61.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Certification of Breeder's Selection
  seed potatoes on a farm unit shall comply with all of the following
  conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the parental material has been tested and not found
    positive for potato spindle tuber viroid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the lot or growing crop has been
    subjected to laboratory tests and not found positive for the
    presence of [Clavibacter michiganensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} subsp.
    [sepedonicus]{.otherLang lang="la"};

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the application for inspection has been completed
    in accordance with section 49;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all fees have been paid in accordance with section
    62;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Breeder's Selection seed potatoes are not grown
    within 10 m of any class of seed potatoes;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Breeder's Selection seed potatoes are inspected
    at least twice during the growing season; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Breeder's Selection seed potatoes have met the
    standards set out in subsection 47.7(3) for Foundation seed
    potatoes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The movement of Breeder's Selection seed potatoes
  shall be in containers of 20 kg or less and are otherwise subject to
  the requirements set out in subsection 54(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-198, s. 20
:::

### [Fees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511412 .Subheading}

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  fee that a grower shall pay for an inspection under this Part is the
  applicable fee provided for in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency
  Fees Notice.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee shall be submitted with the form referred to
  in subsection 49(1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No portion of a fee referred to in subsection (1) is
  refundable after an inspector has begun an inspection of a crop in
  respect of which the fee was paid.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A crop certificate will not be issued for any fields
  under cultivation by that grower until all applicable fees have been
  paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/85-903, s. 7
- SOR/91-526, s. 3
- SOR/93-331, s. 3
- SOR/2000-183, s. 42
- SOR/2002-198, s. 21
:::

**[[62.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62.1 .sectionLabel}** No fees set out in
the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice are payable by a grower
in the Province of Newfoundland with respect to crops grown in
Newfoundland.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-331, s. 4
- SOR/95-215, s. 3
- SOR/2000-183, s. 42
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Variety Registration]{.HTitleText1} {#h-511423 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511424 .Subheading}

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[applicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[applicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} resides permanently in Canada,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applies to the Registrar for the registration of
      a variety, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to whom any notice or correspondence under the
      Act and these Regulations may be sent;
      ([demandeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Certificate of Registration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Certificate of Registration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    certificate issued by the Registrar certifying that the variety
    named therein is registered under these Regulations; ([certificat
    d'enregistrement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[list of experts]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[list of experts]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a list established
    pursuant to section 71; ([liste d'experts]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[merit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[merit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to a variety, that
    the variety is equal or superior to appropriate reference varieties
    with regard to any single characteristic or combination of
    characteristics that renders the variety beneficial for a particular
    use in a specific area of Canada; ([valeur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[recommending committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recommending committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a committee
    that is approved by the Minister under section 65.1; ([comité de
    recommandation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a province, a group of
    provinces, the Canadian Wheat Board Area or that part of Canada
    outside the Canadian Wheat Board Area; ([région]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person to whom a
    Certificate of Registration is issued; ([titulaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person designated by the
    President to register varieties; ([registraire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[representative reference sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[representative reference sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    sample of seed that is submitted in support of registration of a
    variety and guaranteed by the owner or breeder of the variety as
    truly representing the variety. ([échantillon de référence
    représentatif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2000-184, s. 86
- SOR/2009-186, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511440 .Subheading}

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of seed and seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511444 .Subheading}

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** Every variety, other than
a variety of a species, kind or type listed in Schedule III, is exempted
from the prohibition set out in paragraph 3(1)(b) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/93-162, s. 9
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-199, s. 1
:::

### [Recommending Committees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511448 .Subheading}

- **[[65.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall approve, for
  Canada or a region of Canada, a committee to establish and administer
  protocols for testing the varieties of a species, kind or type of crop
  listed in Part I of Schedule III, to determine the merit of the
  varieties and to make recommendations respecting their registration if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the members of the committee have the knowledge and
    expertise required to establish and administer testing protocols for
    varieties of that species, kind or type of crop;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the members of the committee have the knowledge and
    expertise required to determine the merit of the varieties of that
    species, kind or type of crop;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the testing protocols established by the committee
    are appropriate for that species, kind or type of crop, are
    practical and are based on scientific principles;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the procedures established by the committee for
    determining the merit of varieties of that species, kind or type of
    crop are appropriate for that purpose and are based on scientific
    principles;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the operating procedures established by the
    committee will ensure that its functioning is transparent and that
    varieties are dealt with in a fair and consistent manner; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} no other committee is approved as a recommending
    committee for that species, kind or type of crop for Canada or the
    region.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall approve, for Canada or a region of
  Canada, a committee to establish and administer protocols for testing
  the varieties of a species, kind or type of crop listed in Part II of
  Schedule III and to make recommendations respecting their registration
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the members of the committee have the knowledge and
    expertise required to establish and administer testing protocols for
    varieties of that species, kind or type of crop;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the testing protocols established by the committee
    are appropriate for that species, kind or type of crop, are
    practical and are based on scientific principles;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operating procedures established by the
    committee will ensure that its functioning is transparent and that
    varieties are dealt with in a fair and consistent manner; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} no other committee is approved as a recommending
    committee for that species, kind or type of crop for Canada or the
    region.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In carrying out its functions, a recommending
  committee must apply the testing protocols it has established, act in
  accordance with its operating procedures and, in the case of a
  committee approved under subsection (1), apply the procedures it has
  established to determine the merit of varieties.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections 67(1) and 67.1(1),
  the recommendation of a recommending committee must be based on the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a species, kind or type of crop that
    is listed in Part I of Schedule III, the results of testing the
    variety in accordance with the relevant testing protocols and a
    determination of whether the variety has merit; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a species, kind or type of crop that
    is listed in Part II of Schedule III, the results of testing the
    variety in accordance with the relevant testing protocols.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-186, s. 2
:::

### [Condition of Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511468 .Subheading}

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** No variety may be
registered unless an application for the registration of the variety is
made to the Registrar under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/93-162, s. 10
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Applications for Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511472 .Subheading}

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for the registration of a variety shall be signed by the
  applicant and shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contain the following information, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the proposed variety name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the scientific and common names of the kind or
      species to which the variety belongs,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the pedigree, origin, history
      and methods of development of the variety,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the variety including,
      where applicable, morphological, agronomic, pathological,
      physiological and biochemical characteristics,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a recommendation that is not more than two years
      old from a recommending committee stating whether the variety
      should be registered,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the results of the testing on which the
      recommendation is based,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the variety is sold
      in other countries and, if so, the name or names under which it is
      sold,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the arrangements for the
      maintenance of seed stocks, and

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the Canadian
      representative of the variety; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where the applicant is a person other than the
      breeder or owner of the variety, a signed statement from the
      breeder or owner of the variety indicating that the applicant has
      been authorized by the breeder or owner of the variety to apply
      for the registration of the variety in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for every variety except varieties of potatoes,
      a representative reference sample of seed of that variety, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for potatoes, a set of photographic slides or
      digital images that detail plant morphology.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraphs (1)(a)(v) and (vi) do not apply in
  respect of a species, kind or type of crop that is listed in Part III
  of Schedule III.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar may, in addition to the information
  referred to in subsection (1), require that the applicant provide such
  further information as may be necessary to determine the merit and
  identity of the variety.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The applicant or the registrant, as the case may be,
  shall provide the Registrar with any information pertaining to the
  genetic purity or the utility of a variety which differs in any way
  from the information supplied in support of the application for
  registration, forthwith upon receiving the information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2009-186, s. 3
- SOR/2014-114, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20090618.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Eligibility Requirements for Variety Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511494 .Subheading}

- **[[67.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  variety of a species, kind or type of crop that is listed in Part I of
  Schedule III is eligible for registration if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the variety has merit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the variety has been tested in accordance with the
    testing protocols of a recommending committee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the recommending committee has made a
    recommendation respecting registration of the variety;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety or its progeny is not detrimental to
    human or animal health and safety or the environment when grown and
    used as intended;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the representative reference sample of the variety
    does not contain off-types or impurities in excess of the
    Association's standards for varietal purity;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the variety meets the standards for varietal purity
    established by the Association or these Regulations for a variety of
    that species, kind or type;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the variety is distinguishable from all other
    varieties that were or currently are registered in Canada;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the variety name is not a registered trademark in
    respect of the variety;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the variety name is not likely to mislead a
    purchaser with respect to the composition, genetic origin or utility
    of the variety;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the variety name is not likely to be confused with
    the name of a variety that was or currently is registered;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the variety name is not likely to offend the
    public;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} no false statement or falsified document and no
    misleading or incorrect information have been submitted in support
    of the application for registration; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the information provided to the Registrar is
    sufficient to enable the variety to be evaluated.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A variety of a species, kind or type of crop that is
  listed in Part II of Schedule III is eligible for registration if the
  requirements for eligibility set out in paragraphs (1)(b) to (m) are
  met.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A variety of a species, kind or type of crop that is
  listed in Part III of Schedule III is eligible for registration if the
  requirements for eligibility set out in paragraphs (1)(d) to (m) are
  met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-186, s. 4
:::

### [Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511514 .Subheading}

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  if the requirements set out in sections 67 and 67.1 are met, the
  Registrar shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} register the variety by entering in the Register of
    Varieties

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the variety name of the seed,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the kind or species of seed,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the registration number,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the registration date, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} where appropriate, the information referred to in
      section 69; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue a Certificate of Registration to the
    applicant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall make the registration of a
  variety subject to the following terms and conditions, where
  applicable:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a variety of a species, kind or type
    of crop that is listed in Part I or II of Schedule III, if a minimum
    of one year of testing demonstrates that the variety may be eligible
    for registration but that further testing is required before a final
    decision can be rendered, the registration shall be limited to an
    initial period of not more than three years that shall be extended
    on written request by the applicant if eligibility for registration
    continues to be demonstrated, but under no circumstances shall the
    total duration of the registration exceed five years;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the disease susceptibility or inferior
    quality characteristics of the variety may have an adverse effect in
    a particular region on the identity of other varieties registered
    for that region or where in a particular region the variety or
    progeny thereof may be detrimental to human or animal health and
    safety or the environment, the registration of the variety shall be
    restricted to that part of Canada outside that region; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the biochemical or biophysical
    characteristics of a variety distinguish it from the majority of
    registered varieties of the same kind or species and it may have an
    adverse effect on the identity of those registered varieties, the
    registrant shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} establish and maintain a quality control system
      for the management of potentially adverse effects of the variety,
      including management responsibility, contract review, product
      identification and traceability, inspection, testing, control of
      nonconforming product, corrective and preventive actions, records
      and training of personnel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} describe the quality control system in a
      document and submit the document and any subsequent amendments to
      that document to the Registrar for review and approval,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} implement the quality control system, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} agree in writing, for the purpose of verifying
      compliance with subparagraph (iii), to provide the Registrar with
      information relating to the distribution, use and disposition of
      any seed of the variety or any progeny thereof.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Registrar makes the registration of a
  variety subject to any of the terms and conditions referred to in
  paragraph (2)(c), the Registrar, where applicable, shall make the
  registration subject to the following additional terms and conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each vendor, purchaser and importer of a variety in
    respect of which paragraph (2)(c) applies shall consent to grow only
    a crop from that seed using sufficient isolation distances as
    specified for the production of seed of certified status for that
    kind or species in the Regulations and Procedures for Pedigreed Seed
    Crop Production published by the Association, as amended from time
    to time; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the variety is visually indistinguishable
    from registered varieties for which the Canadian Grain Commission
    has established by regulation official grades and grade names with
    designated specifications, but the variety does not meet those
    specifications, the production, harvest and delivery of the crop
    shall be documented to ensure that all of the seed and its progeny
    can be accounted for.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall publish a list of all varieties
  registered under this Part once a year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2009-186, s. 5
- SOR/2012-13, s. 8(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68-20090618.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a registration is made subject to any of the terms and conditions set
  out in subsections 68(2) and (3), the Registrar shall enter those
  terms and conditions in the Register of Varieties and shall mark on
  the face of the Certificate of Registration the word "RESTRICTED" or
  "LIMITÉ\".

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the requirements of subsection (1),
  the Registrar may mark any of the terms and conditions set out in
  subsections 68(2) and (3) on the face of the Certificate of
  Registration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** Where the situation that
required the imposition of any of the terms or conditions set out in
subsections 68(2) and (3) no longer exists, the Registrar shall enter
the cancellation of the term or condition in the Register of Varieties
and shall issue a new Certificate of Registration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [List of Experts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511546 .Subheading}

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Registrar must annually establish a list of experts consisting of not
  less than six and not more than twenty persons whose professional
  qualifications, experience or achievements in the field of seed or
  variety development and evaluation render them experts for the
  purposes of advising the Registrar on matters involving the refusal or
  cancellation of any registration under this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not enter the name of any person
  on the list of experts unless that person consents to advise the
  Registrar, on request, on matters involving the refusal or
  cancellation of any registration under this Part without charging any
  fees or claiming any disbursements.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2014-114, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2009-186, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Review]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511553 .Subheading}

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Registrar refuses to register a variety or registers the variety
  subject to any terms and conditions referred to in subsection 68(2) or
  (3), the Registrar shall send a notice, by registered mail, to the
  applicant giving the reasons therefor and advising that the applicant
  may request that the Registrar review the decision.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice referred to in subsection (1) is deemed to
  be received by the applicant on the seventh day after the day on which
  the notice was mailed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who receives a notice referred to in
  subsection (1) may, within thirty days after the day on which the
  notice is received, make a written request to the Registrar for a
  review of the decision.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The request referred to in subsection (3) shall
  include the reason why the applicant believes that the Registrar
  should review the decision and may include or be accompanied by such
  documentation or information as the applicant deems appropriate.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the Registrar receives a request referred to in
  subsection (3), the Registrar shall review the decision.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The review referred to in subsection (5) shall be
  conducted as informally and expeditiously as possible and in such a
  way as to permit the applicant to present the applicant's case and to
  reply to any evidence, but shall not include oral representations
  unless the Registrar determines that they are necessary to assist the
  Registrar in the review.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where, during the course of a review, the Registrar
  chooses to be advised by an expert, or the applicant who has requested
  the review requests that the Registrar be so advised, the Registrar
  shall select from the list of experts a person who is available and
  who has no interest in the outcome of the review.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Where no person from the list of experts is selected
  because of non-availability or an interest in the outcome of the
  review, the Registrar may select any person who qualifies to be
  entered on the list of experts.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} On completion of the review, the Registrar shall
  immediately notify, by registered mail, the applicant who requested
  the review of the decision made by the Registrar, including the
  reasons therefor.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Cancellation of Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511566 .Subheading}

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar must cancel
the registration of a variety if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the variety has demonstrated susceptibility to
  disease or such inferior quality as to have an adverse effect on
  Canadian agriculture and the food system;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the variety has demonstrated significant levels of
  contamination such that the genetic purity of the variety has been
  jeopardized;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the variety has been altered in such a manner that it
  differs from the representative reference sample;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the variety or its progeny may be detrimental to
  human or animal health and safety or the environment;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was submitted in
  support of the application for registration;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the variety name became a registered trademark in
  respect of that variety after registration;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the variety has been altered in such a manner as to
  convert it to a variety that is registered under a different name;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the variety has been found to be indistinguishable
  from another variety that was or currently is registered in Canada;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the variety is no longer subject to the variety
  registration requirements; or

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the registrant has requested the cancellation of the
  registration of the variety.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2009-186, s. 7
- SOR/2014-114, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20090618.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Registrar must not cancel the registration of a variety unless the
  Registrar sends a notice, by registered mail, to the registrant giving
  the reasons for the cancellation and advising that the registrant may
  make representations concerning the cancellation to the Registrar in
  accordance with this section.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the registrant has
  requested the cancellation of the variety.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice referred to in subsection (1) is deemed to
  be received by the registrant on the seventh day after the day on
  which the notice is mailed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A registrant who receives a notice referred to in
  subsection (1) may, within 30 days after the day on which the notice
  is received, make written representations to the Registrar concerning
  the cancellation.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The procedure set out in subsections 73(3) to (9)
  applies in respect of the review of the representations made under
  subsection (3), with such modifications as the circumstances require.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, on conclusion of the review of the
  representations made under subsection (3), the Registrar determines
  that the registration must be cancelled, the cancellation is effective
  on the seventh day after the day on which the notice of that
  determination is mailed to the registrant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2014-114, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Registrar must reinstate the registration of a variety if the former
  registrant makes a written request to reinstate the registration and
  the Registrar determines that the reasons for cancelling the
  registration are no longer valid.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A former registrant who makes a request to reinstate
  a registration is not required to submit the information referred to
  in section 67.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2014-114, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Change of a Variety Name]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511594 .Subheading}

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** The registrant shall
apply to the Registrar where the registrant wishes to change a variety
name of a variety registered under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Registration of Establishments that Prepare Seed and the Licensing of Operators]{.HTitleText1} {#h-511598 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511599 .Subheading}

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[approved conditioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[approved conditioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an establishment
    that prepares seed of pedigreed status and in respect of which a
    registration as an approved conditioner is in force; ([conditionneur
    agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[authorized importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an establishment
    that prepares imported seed and in respect of which a registration
    as an authorized importer is in force; ([importateur
    autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to seed, means not
    packaged in a fastened container; ([en vrac]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[bulk storage facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk storage facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an establishment
    that stores in bulk seed graded with a Canada pedigreed grade name
    and in respect of which a registration as a bulk storage facility is
    in force; ([installation d'entreposage en vrac]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who applies for
    and is granted registration in respect of an establishment;
    ([détenteur d'agrément]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person designated by the
    President to register establishments and licence operators.
    ([registraire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-849, s. 8
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2000-184, s. 87
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511609 .Subheading}

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of seed potatoes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Registration of an Establishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511613 .Subheading}

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for the registration of an establishment as an approved
  conditioner, an authorized importer or a bulk storage facility shall
  be made in writing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a conformity verification body; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the Registrar, if there is no conformity
    verification body.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When making an application under paragraph (1)(b),
  the applicant shall include the applicable fee set out in the Canadian
  Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 5
:::

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** If an application is made
under section 80 for the registration of an establishment as an approved
conditioner, the establishment has the documents referred to in
subsection 86(1) and the equipment referred to in subsection 86(2) and
either a conformity verification body recommends the registration or, if
there is no conformity verification body, the applicant pays the
applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
Notice, the Registrar shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} register the establishment, for a period ending
  December 31 of the year in respect of which the application is made,
  as an approved conditioner by entering the name of the establishment
  in the Agency's Register of Seed Establishments and by assigning a
  registration number to the establishment; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue a Certificate of Registration in respect of the
  establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-292, s. 39
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 6
:::

**[[81.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81.1 .sectionLabel}** If an application is
made under section 80 for the registration of an establishment as an
authorized importer, the establishment has the documents referred to in
subsection 86(1) and the facilities referred to in subsection 86(3) and
either a conformity verification body recommends the registration or, if
there is no conformity verification body, the applicant pays the
applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
Notice, the Registrar shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} register the establishment, for a period ending
  December 31 of the year in respect of which the application is made,
  as an authorized importer by entering the name of the establishment in
  the Agency's Register of Seed Establishments and by assigning a
  registration number to the establishment; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue a Certificate of Registration in respect of the
  establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-292, s. 40
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 7
:::

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** If an application is made
under section 80 for the registration of an establishment as a bulk
storage facility, the establishment has the documents referred to in
subsection 86(1) and the facilities referred to in subsection 86(3) and
either a conformity verification body recommends the registration or, if
there is no conformity verification body, the applicant pays the
applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
Notice, the Registrar shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} register the establishment, for a period ending
  December 31 of the year in respect of which the application is made,
  as a bulk storage facility by entering the name of the establishment
  in the Agency's Register of Seed Establishments and by assigning a
  registration number to the establishment; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue a Certificate of Registration in respect of the
  establishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-292, s. 41
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 8
:::

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-93,
s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** Unless the registration
of an establishment is suspended or cancelled under section 87 or 88,
and subject to section 92, the Registrar shall renew the registration of
every establishment annually, on the recommendation of a conformity
verification body or, if there is no conformity verification body, on
payment by the establishment, before January 1 of the year in respect of
which the registration is to be renewed, of the applicable renewal fee
set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 10
:::

- **[[84.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a conformity verification body refuses to make a recommendation to the
  Registrar that an establishment be registered as an approved
  conditioner under section 81, as an authorized importer under section
  81.1 or as a bulk storage facility under section 82, or that the
  establishment's registration be renewed under section 84, the
  conformity verification body shall send a notice of that decision to
  the applicant by registered mail, giving the reasons for the decision
  and advising that the applicant may request that the Registrar review
  the decision. The conformity verification body shall also send a copy
  of the notice, including the reasons, to the Registrar.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The applicant may, within 30 days after the day on
  which the notice is received, make a written request to the Registrar
  for a review of the decision.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The request shall include the reason why the
  applicant believes that the Registrar should review the decision and
  may include or be accompanied by any documentation or information that
  the applicant considers appropriate. The request shall also include
  the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
  Notice.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the request, the Registrar shall review
  the decision.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The review shall be conducted as informally and
  expeditiously as possible and in such a way as to permit the applicant
  to present the applicant's case and to reply to any evidence, but
  shall not include oral representations unless the Registrar determines
  that they are necessary.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Registrar finds that the conformity
  verification body should have recommended that the establishment be
  registered as an approved conditioner, an authorized importer or a
  bulk storage facility, or that the establishment's registration be
  renewed, the Registrar shall register the establishment or renew its
  registration, as the case may be, as if the recommendation had been
  made.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall send a notice of the Registrar's
  decision and the reasons for it to the applicant by registered mail.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-93, s. 10
:::

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
registered establishment shall post in a conspicuous place in the
registered establishment the Certificate of Registration issued under
section 81, 81.1, 82 or 83 for the period during which the certificate
remains in force.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Conditions Respecting Registered Establishments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511653 .Subheading}

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  operator of a registered establishment shall keep at the establishment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} up-to-date copies of the [Seeds
    Act](/eng/acts/S-8), these Regulations and all other written
    material that the Agency delivers to the establishment from time to
    time; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a list that identifies

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the measures and documentation required to ensure
      that the establishment is in compliance with the requirements of
      these Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the persons who, where applicable, handle,
      store, sample, test, process, grade, label and document all seed
      in the establishment, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the operators of the establishment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every approved conditioner shall have

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} processing equipment of such construction and
    design that it may be properly cleaned between the processing of
    successive seed lots so as to eliminate residues and prevent
    contamination; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} equipment for drawing representative samples of
    seed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every bulk storage facility and every authorized
  importer shall have adequate storage facilities to enable the
  identification and maintain the identity of different seed lots and to
  prevent cross-contamination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2000-184, s. 88
- SOR/2015-55, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-86-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 86"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Suspension and Cancellation of Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511667 .Subheading}

- **[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 89, the Registrar shall suspend the registration of an
  establishment where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was submitted in
    support of the application for registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the establishment does not comply with a provision
    of the Act, the [Seeds Act](/eng/acts/S-8) or these Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food
    Inspection Agency Fees Notice for a service provided to that
    establishment has not been paid.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not suspend the registration of
  an establishment where, before the registrant is heard pursuant to
  paragraph 89(b), the registrant takes corrective measures and an
  inspector verifies that those measures have been taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/96-273, s. 2
- SOR/2007-223, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-87-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 87"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 89
and 90, the Registrar shall cancel the registration of an establishment
where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registrant does not pay the applicable annual
  renewal fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice
  before January 1 of the year in respect of which the registration is
  to be renewed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registrant maintains any false or misleading
  records or samples in respect of any seed in the establishment;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the registrant falsely represents any seed to be of
  pedigreed status;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the registrant provides false or misleading
  information to an inspector;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the registration of an establishment has been
  suspended three times within a 24 month period; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the suspension of a registration has been in effect
  for one year and the registrant has not yet implemented corrective
  measures.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
:::

**[[88.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88.1 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall
cancel the registration of an establishment at the request of the
registrant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-6, s. 111
:::

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall not
suspend or cancel the registration of an establishment unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector has provided the registrant with a
  written report setting out the reasons for the suspension or
  cancellation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has given the registrant an opportunity
  to be heard, either by written or oral representations, in respect of
  the suspension or cancellation; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has sent a notice of suspension or
  cancellation of registration to the registrant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall not
cancel the registration of an establishment for a reason set out in any
of paragraphs 88(a) to (d) where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registrant establishes that the basis for the
  cancellation was the result of an error and the registrant took
  precautions and exercised due diligence to prevent the occurrence of
  the error;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registrant undertakes to bring the error to the
  attention of the persons likely to be affected by the error by placing
  an announcement in such media, and within such time, not exceeding 30
  days, as the Registrar indicates; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the announcement referred
  to in paragraph (b) was made within the time period indicated by the
  Registrar.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** A suspension of a
registration shall remain in effect until

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the registrant has taken
  corrective measures; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar notifies the registrant in writing that
  the suspension is lifted.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall not,
before the expiration of a period of 24 months following a cancellation
of a registration, accept an application for the registration of an
establishment from a registrant whose registration was cancelled for a
reason set out in any of paragraphs 88(b) to (e).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Operation of Registered Establishments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511708 .Subheading}

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  registered establishment shall have an individual identified as the
  operator of the establishment who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is licensed as an operator pursuant to these
    Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} supervises all operations in the establishment; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is ultimately responsible, where applicable, for
    the proper handling, storage, sampling, testing, processing,
    grading, labelling and documentation of all seed in the
    establishment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall inform the Agency, not less than
  48 hours before shipment, of the first 10 seed lots that are handled
  by the establishment under the supervision of that operator.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall maintain records and samples,
  including certificates of analysis, crop certificates where applicable
  and representative samples, to ascertain the status and grade of every
  seed lot that has been handled by the establishment for a period of
  one year after the final disposition of the seed lot, and, in the case
  of Foundation and Registered status seed, for a period of two years
  after the disposition of the seed lot.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} When seed of pedigreed status is conditioned or
  graded, the operator shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} complete, within 30 days after the conditioning or
    grading, a pedigreed seed declaration that contains sufficient
    information to permit the tracing of all claims made with respect to
    the seed, including the name of the grower, crop certificate number,
    quantity of seed and official tag use; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep the declaration for a period of one year after
    the final disposition of the seed and, in the case of foundation
    status and registered status seed, for a period of two years after
    the final disposition of the seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2000-184, s. 89
- SOR/2012-13, ss. 7, 8(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-93-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 93"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Operators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511721 .Subheading}

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** No individual shall
operate a registered establishment without being licensed by the
Registrar for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

### [Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511725 .Subheading}

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 97, an individual
  who wishes to be licensed as an operator of a registered establishment
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply for a licence in writing to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a conformity verification body, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar, if there is no conformity
      verification body; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} submit to an evaluation that is set by the
    Registrar that measures knowledge of the principles and practices
    for the handling, storage, labelling and documentation of seed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the evaluation referred to in
  paragraph (1)(b), an individual who wishes to be licensed as an
  operator of an approved conditioner shall submit to an evaluation that
  is set by the Registrar that measures the individual's knowledge of
  the principles and practices for the preparing, sampling, testing and
  grading of seed of pedigreed status.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the evaluation referred to in
  paragraph (1)(b), an individual who wishes to be licensed as an
  operator of an authorized importer shall submit to an evaluation that
  is set by the Registrar that measures the individual's knowledge of
  the requirements for importing seed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 11
- SOR/2003-6, s. 112
:::

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall, on
the recommendation of a conformity verification body or, if there is no
conformity verification body, on payment of the applicable fee set out
in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice, issue a licence to
the individual as an operator of an approved conditioner, a bulk storage
facility or an authorized importer, as the case may be, if the
individual has obtained a mark of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 80 per cent on the evaluations referred to
  in paragraphs 95(1)(b) and (2)(a), and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 90 per cent on the evaluation referred to in
  paragraph 95(2)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 12
:::

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-93,
s. 13\]]{.Repealed}

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** Unless a licence is
suspended or cancelled under section 99 or 100, and subject to section
104, the Registrar shall renew the licence of an operator annually, on
the recommendation of a conformity verification body or, if there is no
conformity verification body, on the payment by the operator, before
January 1 of the year in respect of which the licence is to be renewed,
of the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency
Fees Notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
- SOR/2001-93, s. 14
:::

- **[[98.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a conformity verification body refuses to make a recommendation to the
  Registrar that an individual be licensed under section 96 or that an
  individual's licence be renewed under section 98, the conformity
  verification body shall send a notice of that decision to the
  applicant by registered mail, giving the reasons for the decision and
  advising that the applicant may request that the Registrar review the
  decision. The conformity verification body shall also send a copy of
  the notice, including the reasons, to the Registrar.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The applicant may, within 30 days after the day on
  which the notice is received, make a written request to the Registrar
  for a review of the decision.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The request shall include the reasons why the
  applicant believes that the Registrar should review the decision and
  may include or be accompanied by any documentation or information that
  the applicant considers appropriate. The request shall also include
  the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees
  Notice.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the request, the Registrar shall review
  the decision.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The review shall be conducted as informally and
  expeditiously as possible and in such a way as to permit the applicant
  to present the applicant's case and to reply to any evidence, but
  shall not include oral representations unless the Registrar determines
  that they are necessary.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Registrar finds that the conformity
  verification body should have recommended that the individual be
  licensed, or that the individual's licence be renewed, the Registrar
  shall license the individual or renew the individual's licence, as the
  case may be, as if the recommendation had been made.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall send a notice of the Registrar's
  decision and the reasons for it to the applicant by registered mail.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-93, s. 14
:::

### [Suspension and Cancellation of Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511755 .Subheading}

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 101, the Registrar shall suspend the licence of an operator
  where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information has been submitted
    in support of the application for a licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator does not comply with a provision of
    the Act, the [Seeds Act](/eng/acts/S-8) or these Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicable fee set out in the Canadian Food
    Inspection Agency Fees Notice for a service provided to that
    operator has not been paid.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Registrar shall not suspend the licence of an
  operator where, before the operator is heard pursuant to paragraph
  101(b), the operator takes corrective measures and an inspector
  verifies that those measures have been taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/96-273, s. 3
- SOR/2007-223, s. 24
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-99-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 99"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 101
and 102, the Registrar shall cancel the licence of an operator where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operator does not pay the applicable annual
  renewal fee set out in the Canadian Food Inspection Agency Fees Notice
  before January 1 of the year in respect of which the licence is to be
  renewed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator maintains any false or misleading
  records or samples in respect of any seed in the establishment;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operator falsely represents any seed to be of
  pedigreed status;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the operator provides false or misleading information
  to an inspector;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the licence of the operator has been suspended three
  times within a 24 month period; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the suspension of a licence has been in effect for
  one year and the operator has not yet implemented corrective measures.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/97-534, s. 5
- SOR/2000-183, s. 43
:::

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall not
suspend or cancel the licence of an operator unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector has provided the operator with a written
  report setting out the reasons for the suspension or cancellation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has given the operator an opportunity
  to be heard, either by written or oral representations, in respect of
  the suspension or cancellation; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar has sent a notice of suspension or
  cancellation of licence to the operator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** The Registrar shall not
cancel the licence of an operator for a reason set out in any of
paragraphs 100(a) to (d) where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operator establishes that the basis for the
  cancellation was the result of an error and the operator took
  precautions and exercised due diligence to prevent the occurrence of
  the error;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator undertakes to bring the error to the
  attention of the persons likely to be affected by it by placing an
  announcement in such media, and within such time, not exceeding 30
  days, as the Registrar indicates; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the announcement referred
  to in paragraph (b) was made within the time period indicated by the
  Registrar.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** A suspension of a
licence shall remain in effect until

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspector verifies that the operator has taken
  corrective measures; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Registrar notifies the operator in writing that
  the suspension is lifted.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
:::

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Registrar shall not renew the licence of an operator whose licence has
  been suspended three times, unless, after the third suspension, the
  operator has successfully completed the applicable evaluations
  referred to in section 95.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-93, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2001-93, s. 15
:::

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** If an individual's
operator's licence has been cancelled for a reason set out in any of
paragraphs 100(b) to (e), the Registrar shall not issue an operator's
licence to the individual again unless 24 months have gone by since the
cancellation and the individual satisfies the conditions referred to in
sections 95 and 96.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-252, s. 3
- SOR/2001-93, s. 16
:::

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/97-534, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Release of Seed]{.HTitleText1} {#h-511799 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511800 .Subheading}

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Part,

  [[confined release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[confined release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means release under
      conditions intended to minimize the establishment and spread, in
      the environment, of seed or of genetic material from plants
      derived from the seed, and the interaction of the seed or genetic
      material with the environment; ([dissémination en milieu
      confiné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the components of the
      Earth and includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} air, land and water,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all layers of the atmosphere,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all organic and inorganic matter and living
        organisms, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the interacting natural systems that include
        components referred to in paragraphs (a) to (c);
        ([environnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[novel trait]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[novel trait]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of seed, means a
      characteristic of the seed that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been intentionally selected, created or
        introduced into a distinct, stable population of cultivated seed
        of the same species through a specific genetic change, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} based on valid scientific rationale, is not
        substantially equivalent, in terms of its specific use and
        safety both for the environment and for human health, to any
        characteristic of a distinct, stable population of cultivated
        seed of the same species in Canada, having regard to weediness
        potential, gene flow, plant pest potential, impact on non-target
        organisms and impact on biodiversity; ([caractère
        nouveau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any discharge or emission of
      seed into the environment or exposure of seed to the environment
      and includes the growing and field testing of plants;
      ([dissémination]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unconfined release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unconfined release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means release on an
      unrestricted basis. ([dissémination en milieu
      ouvert]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, seed is toxic if it is
  entering or may enter the environment in a quantity or concentration
  or under conditions that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have or may have an immediate or long-term harmful
    effect on the environment or its biological diversity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} constitute or may constitute a danger to the
    environment on which life depends; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} constitute or may constitute a danger in Canada to
    human life or health.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
- SOR/2001-274, s. 1
:::

### [Exemptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511820 .Subheading}

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** The following seed is
exempt from the operation of this Part:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} seed grown in Canada outside of containment before
  the coming into force of this Part in such a manner that the seed
  constitutes a distinct, stable population in the Canadian environment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} seed grown in containment in such a manner that there
  is no release into the environment of any genetic material from the
  plants derived from the seed; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} seed that is derived from seed referred to in
  paragraph (a), or from seed in respect of which an unconfined release
  has been authorized under paragraph 111(1)(b), and that is
  substantially equivalent, in terms of its specific use and safety both
  for the environment and for human health, to seed of the same species,
  having regard to weediness potential, gene flow, plant pest potential,
  impact on non-target organisms and impact on biodiversity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
:::

### [Notification and Authorization]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511827 .Subheading}

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4),
  no person shall undertake either the confined release or unconfined
  release of seed unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notification of the proposed release has been
    provided in writing to the Minister, accompanied by the information
    set out in section 110; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has authorized the release under
    section 111.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of seed of a
  variety that is registered under Part III.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an
  unconfined release that was authorized by the Minister before the
  coming into force of this Part.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the accompanying information that is required
  to be provided to the Minister pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) in respect
  of a proposed confined release is the same as information that has
  already been provided to the Minister before the coming into force of
  this Part, or that is provided on or after the coming into force of
  this Part in relation to another notification or authorization, the
  information is not required to be provided again.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
- SOR/2001-274, s. 2
:::

### [Information Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511837 .Subheading}

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the
  accompanying information that is required to be provided to the
  Minister by a person pursuant to paragraph 109(1)(a) is the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or body responsible for the
    proposed release and the name of the person who will be in charge of
    carrying out the release;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purpose and objectives of the proposed release;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the plants derived from the unmodified host seed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of seed with a novel trait,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the novel trait expressed by the plants derived
      from the modified seed and, where the novel trait was introduced
      from another species, details of the donor organism and of the
      methods of incorporation of the novel trait into the host seed,
      where applicable, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the plants derived from the modified seed
      resulting from the introduction of the novel trait, including
      details relating to expression of the novel trait and the
      stability of the incorporation of the novel trait into the plants
      derived from the modified seed, and a comparison of the
      characteristics of the plants derived from the modified seed with
      those derived from the unmodified host seed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} all other information and test data in respect of
    the plants referred to in paragraph (d) that are relevant to
    identifying the risk to the environment, including the risk to human
    health, and that are in the person's possession or to which the
    person ought reasonably to have access;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} other government agencies, either Canadian or
    foreign, that have been provided with information in respect of the
    plants referred to in subparagraph (d)(ii), and the purpose for
    which the information was provided; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the analytical methodologies
    followed in generating any submitted data, including quality control
    and quality assurance procedures.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the information referred to in
  subsection (1) and subject to subsection (4), the following
  accompanying information shall be provided to the Minister with any
  notification in respect of a proposed confined release:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposed starting date, completion date and
    site of the confined release; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proposed confined release protocol, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any confinement measures intended to mitigate the
      establishment and spread, in the environment, of the seed or of
      genetic material from plants derived from the seed, and the
      interaction of the seed or genetic material with the environment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the monitoring plan and procedures to be
      followed both during and after the confined release,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the method to be used for the safe disposal of
      the seed and all progeny and plant material, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the contingency plan to minimize any adverse
      effect of an accidental movement, outside of the confined release
      site, of the seed or genetic material from plants derived from the
      seed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the information referred to in
  subsection (1) and subject to subsection (4), data describing
  potential interactions of the seed, or plants derived from the seed,
  with other life forms and an evaluation of the potential risk of harm
  posed to the environment, including the risk of harm posed to human
  health, as a result of those interactions shall be provided to the
  Minister with any notification in respect of a proposed unconfined
  release.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information required by subparagraph (1)(d)(ii),
  paragraph (1)(e) or (g) or subsection (2) or (3) or any part of that
  information may be omitted if the Minister determines, on the basis of
  a written scientific rationale provided by the person referred to in
  subsection (1), that the information or part is not relevant or cannot
  practicably be obtained and is not required for the Minister's
  decision under section 111, and notifies the person of that
  determination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
:::

### [Decision of the Minister]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511860 .Subheading}

- **[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), on
  receiving the notification provided under paragraph 109(1)(a), the
  Minister shall, after considering all relevant matters, including the
  information provided under that paragraph, and after evaluating the
  potential impact on and risk to the environment, including the
  potential impact on and risk to human health, posed by the proposed
  release

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorize a confined release, where the proposed
    release could pose a risk to the environment, and may, where
    necessary in order to minimize the risk, impose conditions for the
    management of the risk;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorize an unconfined release, where the proposed
    release poses minimal risk to the environment, and may, where
    necessary in order to minimize the risk, impose conditions for the
    management of the risk; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} refuse to authorize the release, where the proposed
    release poses an unacceptable risk to the environment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In evaluating the risk to the environment for the
  purposes of subsection (1), the Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consider such matters as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the effects of the release on the environment,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the magnitude of the exposure to the environment
      of the seed involved in the proposed release; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assess whether the seed is toxic.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may decide not to grant an authorization
  to a person under subsection (1) if the Minister has reasonable
  grounds for believing that the person may not comply with the
  conditions that would be imposed under that subsection in respect of
  the release or the requirements of section 111.1 in respect of the
  release.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
- SOR/2001-274, s. 3
:::

### [Requirements Applying to Confined Release]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511874 .Subheading}

- **[[111.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section,
  [[feed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[livestock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  have the meanings assigned by section 2 of the [Feeds
  Act](/eng/acts/F-9).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is granted an authorization under
  paragraph 111(1)(a) for the confined release of seed with a novel
  trait, or who is in charge of carrying out the confined release,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall prevent the seed and any plants derived from
    the seed from entering into any feed for livestock, or entering into
    any food for humans;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall provide the Agency on request with copies of,
    or allow the Agency to examine,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any contracts that the person enters into or has
      entered into with any persons in relation to the confined release,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's records with respect to the
      confined release, including records of any trials of the seed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall be responsible for all costs of the actions
    required to remedy any situation caused by an accidental release of
    the seed; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} shall be responsible for all costs of collection,
    storage and disposal of the seed and plants derived from the seed if
    the Minister requires the person to stop the confined release.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-274, s. 4
:::

### [New Information Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-511886 .Subheading}

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, at any time after providing
  notification pursuant to paragraph 109(1)(a) or receiving
  authorization pursuant to subsection 111(1), a person becomes aware of
  any new information regarding risk to the environment, including risk
  to human health, that could result from the release, the person shall
  immediately provide the new information to the Minister.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the new information referred
  to in subsection (1) includes new information with respect to the
  matters described in subsection 110(1) if that new information is in
  regard to risk to the environment, including risk to human health,
  that could result from the release.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister, on the basis of the new
  information provided by a person pursuant to subsection (1),
  re-evaluates the potential impact on and risk to the environment,
  including the potential impact on and risk to human health, posed by
  the release and determines that there is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a risk that is less than was apparent at the time
    of the original notification or at the time of any subsequent
    provision of new information since the original notification, the
    Minister may

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where the release has already been authorized,
      maintain the existing conditions respecting the release,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} change the conditions respecting the release, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} remove any of the conditions respecting the
      release;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a risk that is greater than was apparent at the
    time of the original notification or at the time of any subsequent
    provision of new information since the original notification, the
    Minister may

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} impose additional conditions respecting the
      release, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} change the conditions respecting the release; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an unacceptable risk, the Minister shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} refuse to authorize the release, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the release has already been authorized,
      cancel the authorization and require the person to stop the
      release and to take any appropriate action necessary to eliminate
      or minimize the risk.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In re-evaluating the risk to the environment on the
  basis of new information for the purposes of subsection (2), the
  Minister shall consider the matters referred to in paragraph 111(2)(a)
  and make the assessment referred to in paragraph 111(2)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-9, s. 1
- SOR/2001-274, s. 5
:::

### [Table II]{.HTitleText1} {#table-ii .SchedHeadL1}

Applicable to:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Barley, six-row, two-row, hulless --- [Hordeum
  vulgare]{.otherLang lang="la"} L. subsp. [vulgare]{.otherLang
  lang="la"}

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Bean, mung --- [Vigna radiata]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  (L.) Wilczek var. [radiata]{.otherLang lang="la"}

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Buckwheat, common --- [Fagopyrum
  esculentum]{.otherLang lang="la"} Moench

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Buckwheat, tartarian --- [Fagopyrum
  tataricum]{.otherLang lang="la"} (L.) Gaertn.

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Emmer --- [Triticum turgidum]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. subsp. [dicoccum]{.otherLang lang="la"} (Schrank ex
  Schübl.) Thell. (= [T. dicoccum]{.otherLang lang="la"} Schrank ex
  Schübl.)

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Lentil --- [Lens culinaris]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  Medik.

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Lupine, lupin (grain and forage) ---
  [Lupinus]{.otherLang lang="la"} spp.

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Oats including hulless --- [Avena sativa]{.otherLang

### [Table XIV]{.HTitleText1} {#table-xiv .SchedHeadL1}

#### [PART I]{.HTitleText2} {#part-i .SchedHeadL2}

<td class="noBorderBottom">Kentucky bluegrass, creeping red fescue,

velvet bentgrass, rough bluegrass, Canada bluegrass, sheep fescue,
turf-type tall fescue varieties and turf-type perennial ryegrass
varieties</td>
</tr>

<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">2. Group B</th>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Redtop, crested wheatgrass, timothy
(dwarf-type) and annual ryegrass provided none of those species
constitutes more than 30% by weight singly and no more than 40% by
weight combined of the total mixture</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight" scope="row">3. Group C</th>
<td>All other kinds of grass not listed in this Schedule, if those kinds
are indicated on the label</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</figure>

### [Table XIX]{.HTitleText1} {#table-xix .SchedHeadL1}

Applicable to:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Broccoli --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [italica]{.otherLang lang="la"} Plenck

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Brussels sprouts --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [gemmifera]{.otherLang lang="la"} Zenker

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Cabbage --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [capitata]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Cabbage, Chinese --- [Brassica rapa]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. subsp. [chinensis]{.otherLang lang="la"} (L.) Hanelt or
  [B. rapa]{.otherLang lang="la"} L. subsp. [pekinensis]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} (Lour.) Hanelt

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Cauliflower --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [botrytis]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Collards --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [viridis]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Kale --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.
  var. [sabellica]{.otherLang lang="la"} L. or [B. oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [viridis]{.otherLang lang="la"}

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Kohlrabi --- [Brassica oleracea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [gongylodes]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} Mustard greens --- [Brassica juncea]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} (L.) Czern.

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} Radish --- [Raphanus sativus]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  L.

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} Rape, forage --- [Brassica napus]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [napus]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [B.
  rapa]{.otherLang lang="la"} L. subsp. [campestris]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} (L.) A.R. Clapham

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} Rutabaga (swede) --- [Brassica napus]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [napobrassica]{.otherLang lang="la"} (L.) Reichb.

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} Spinach mustard --- [Brassica rapa]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} L. var. [perviridis]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.H. Bailey

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} Turnip --- [Brassica rapa]{.otherLang lang="la"} L.
  subsp. [rapa]{.otherLang lang="la"}

Definition of Terms Used in Column 4

[[Mature, well cured]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Mature, well cured]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that the sets have
    necks that are well dried out and have not more than 1% and 2% thick
    necks in Canada No. 1 and Canada No. 2 sets, respectively.

[[Sound]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Sound]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that the set is firm and neat
    and will yield only slightly to moderate pressure.

[[Practically free from tops, dirt, leaves or other foreign matter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Practically free from tops, dirt, leaves or other foreign
    matter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means free for all intents and
    purposes. Tops, dirt, leaves or other foreign matter should not
    exceed 1% by weight and not more that 25% of the sets should have
    tops 6.5 cm long or longer.

[[Practically free from disease, decay and moulds and from insect, mechanical, frost or other damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Practically free from disease, decay and moulds and from insect,
    mechanical, frost or other damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that
    not more than 3% combined of the bulbs should be so affected.

[[Practically free from sprouted bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Practically free from sprouted bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    that, for yellow sets, not more than 3% have visible sprouts and
    that, for red, white and globe sets, not more than 6% have visible
    sprouts.

[[Practically free from soft bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Practically free from soft bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that
    soft bulbs should not be in excess of 3%.

[[Reasonably free from tops, dirt, leaves or other foreign matter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Reasonably free from tops, dirt, leaves or other foreign
    matter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that not more than 35% of the
    bulbs should have tops over 6.5 cm long and dirt, leaves or other
    foreign matter should not exceed 2% by weight.

[[Reasonably free from disease, decay and moulds and from insect, mechanical, frost or other damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Reasonably free from disease, decay and moulds and from insect,
    mechanical, frost or other damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that
    not more than 4% combined of the bulbs should be so affected.

[[Reasonably free from soft bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Reasonably free from soft bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that
    not more than 5% combined of the bulbs should be so affected.

[[Sprouted bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Sprouted bulbs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means those bulbs with
    sprouts exceeding 0.6 cm in length.

### [Table XXII]{.HTitleText1} {#table-xxii .SchedHeadL1}

Applicable to all kinds of seeds referred to in Tables I to XX except
those referred to in Tables III and XIII to XV:

- [1. ]{.lawlabel} Canada Foundation Substandard seed is seed of
  Foundation status that meets the standards for purity for Canada
  Foundation No. 2 grade seed.

- [2. ]{.lawlabel} Canada Registered Substandard seed is seed of
  Registered status that meets the standards for purity for Canada
  Registered No. 2 grade seed.

- [3. ]{.lawlabel} Canada Certified Substandard seed is seed of
  Certified status that meets the standards for purity for Canada
  Certified No. 2 grade seed.

- [4]{.lawlabel}  Canada Foundation Substandard (Purity) seed is seed of
  Foundation status that meets the standards for purity for common grade
  seed.
